Motivation: To create a natural, responsive, and trustworthy AI teammate in PUBG: BATTLEGROUNDS that can interact verbally with players in real time while maintaining low-latency performance and safe communication.

Method: Combines a language-model agent for high-level reasoning and decision-making with a fast control layer for real-time action execution. Uses controlled interfaces to process game state, interpret player speech, manage context, and generate synchronized speech and actions. Trained on 39k real-player sessions with full recording of gameplay, speech, decisions, and feedback. Iterative refinement based on player preference and feedback. Deployed with on-device optimization, safety measures, and runtime safeguards.

Result: PUBG Ally successfully functioned as a live teammate in global deployment. Player surveys from 141 countries showed positive responses exceeded negative ones by 25.1 percentage points in recommendation likelihood. Players frequently described Ally not just as a tool but as a companion or teammate, indicating successful social integration.

Conclusion: PUBG Ally demonstrates that autonomous, voice-enabled AI teammates are feasible in complex real-time games when combining advanced language models with efficient real-time control, rigorous training on human-AI interaction data, and careful safety and latency engineering. The agent’s acceptance as a social partner highlights its potential for future interactive gaming ecosystems.

Abstract: We introduce PUBG Ally, an embodied agent for PUBG: BATTLEGROUNDS that can reason, act autonomously, and play alongside players as a voice-enabled teammate. Building such a teammate requires combining two difficult capabilities: it must perceive and respond to a constantly changing game world under strict latency constraints while interacting naturally with players, keeping its speech synchronized with its actions. Ally therefore combines agentic tool use with real-time game control. A language-model agent uses a controlled interface to inspect game information, interpret player speech, maintain context, decide what to say, and issue high-level action choices that steer a faster control layer for movement, combat, and recovery. Because the player's and Ally's speech and actions continually shape each other and the course of the match, training requires data from actual gameplay. We therefore collect data across nearly 39k sessions in which real players play alongside Ally, recording gameplay, player speech, agent decisions, tool use, actions, and player feedback, and use these records for iterative training. To evaluate teammate quality, we use player feedback and preference comparisons to identify gaps between offline evaluations and player preferences, and iteratively refine the evaluation criteria. Deploying Ally in live service further requires low-latency on-device execution and safeguards for player-facing communication, which we address through model compression, context compaction, targeted safety training, runtime guardrails, and memory redaction. During the live service, we surveyed players in 141 countries. Among respondents whose play with Ally was confirmed in game records, positive responses exceeded negative responses by 25.1 percentage points when asked whether they would recommend Ally, with players describing Ally not only as a tool but also as a teammate or companion.

</details>


### [88] [A Risk-Adaptive and Evidence-Constrained Framework for Generative AI Feedback in Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29874)
*Shihao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种风险自适应、证据约束的框架，用于在编程学习中实现个性化支持。基于215名学生2993次失败提交数据，构建了学生独立的预测模型，生成四种匹配的反馈条件，并通过校准风险制定容量受限的干预策略。验证后的逻辑回归模型在测试集上达到0.550的精确率-召回率曲线下面积和0.681的受试者工作特征曲线下面积。更全面的学生历史数据提升了对未修改重交行为的预测能力。标准化修复与证据门控后，519/544条新生成消息包含所有必要组件。固定阈值序列策略选择了17.8%的可干预状态，捕获了25.2%的持续失败案例。结果支持一种证据门控的渐进式辅助策略：校准风险决定干预时机，记录证据限制反馈内容，辅助从自我检查逐步升级至局部提示。该框架将预测、决策与生成过程连接起来，同时保持各环节评估结果的独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能能够将学习分析转化为个性化支持，但反馈系统需解决何时干预、依据何种证据以及提供多大程度帮助等问题。现有方法缺乏对干预时机与反馈内容的合理约束，导致支持效果不佳。因此需要一种既能精准预测学习风险，又能根据证据动态调整反馈策略的系统化框架。

Method: 采用风险自适应、证据约束的框架，利用215名学生2993次失败提交数据训练学生独立的预测模型；通过匹配反馈条件生成136个案例；使用校准风险建立容量受限的干预策略；结合标准化修复与证据门控机制生成反馈消息；并通过固定阈值序列策略评估干预效率。

Result: 验证后的逻辑回归模型在测试集上表现出0.550的精确率-召回率曲线下面积和0.681的受试者工作特征曲线下面积；更完整的学生历史数据提升了对未修改重交行为的预测能力；519/544条新生成消息包含全部必要成分；固定阈值策略选择17.8%的可干预状态，捕获25.2%的持续失败案例。

Conclusion: 研究支持一种证据门控的渐进式辅助策略：校准风险指导干预时机，记录证据约束反馈内容，辅助从自我检查逐步过渡到局部提示。该框架有效整合了预测、决策与生成环节，且各环节评估结果保持独立，为生成式智能辅导系统提供了可扩展的设计范式。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence can turn learning analytics into personalized support, but feedback systems must decide when to intervene, which evidence to use, and how much assistance to provide. We developed a risk-adaptive, evidence-constrained framework for introductory programming using 2993 failed-submission states from 215 students. Student-disjoint models predicted persistent failure and related outcomes; four matched feedback conditions were generated for 136 cases; and calibrated risk informed capacity-limited intervention policies. The validation-selected logistic regression model achieved a test precision-recall area under the curve of 0.550 and a receiver operating characteristic area under the curve of 0.681. Broader student histories improved prediction of unmodified resubmission. After standardized repair and evidence gating, 519 of 544 newly generated messages contained all required components. A fixed-threshold sequential policy selected 17.8% of eligible test states and captured 25.2% of observed persistent failures. These findings support an evidence-gated progressive assistance strategy: calibrated risk guides intervention timing, recorded evidence constrains feedback content, and assistance progresses from self-checks to localized hints when warranted. The framework connects prediction, decision-making, and grounded generation while keeping their evaluation outcomes distinct.

</details>


### [89] [When Can Agents Forget Their Reasoning? ICLR for Long-Horizon Agent Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29875)
*Mingxuan Wang,Fei Luo,Bo Wang,Guorun Yao,Yinglong Guo,Chao Ning,Hongyue Chen,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICLR是一种无需训练的在线方法，通过冻结的代理熵对推理块进行排序，以安全地遗忘长期推理中的冗余内容，同时保留关键动作、工具调用和观察结果。在260个WorkBuddyBench任务上，该方法将平均奖励从0.699提升至0.718，并显著降低输入、输出和缓存读取的令牌数量（分别减少25.5%、14.4%和33.3%）。实验表明，一旦任务相关状态被可靠地外部化为代码、文件或环境反馈，历史推理就变得可替代，揭示了代理推理作为动态工作状态的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期语言模型代理会持续积累推理历史，导致上下文长度和推理成本不断增加，即使早期决策已执行并被观察。传统的静态思维链压缩方法无法处理动态交互中因删除历史推理而可能引发的未来行为改变问题，因此需要一种能够安全遗忘历史推理的方法。

Method: 提出Interaction Aware Compression for Long Horizon Reasoning (ICLR)，利用冻结的代理熵对推理块进行评分，选择性地压缩或遗忘非关键推理内容，同时保持动作、工具调用和观察信息不变，实现无训练的在线压缩。

Result: 在260个WorkBuddyBench任务中，ICLR将平均奖励从0.699提升至0.718，同时减少输入、输出和缓存读取令牌25.5%、14.4%和33.3%；消融实验发现存在轨迹放大效应，即局部推理删除会引起后续计算的非线性变化；进一步分析表明，当任务相关状态被外部化后，历史推理的可替代性增强。

Conclusion: 代理推理应被视为动态工作状态而非永久交互历史，历史推理可在任务状态被可靠外部化后安全遗忘，ICLR有效实现了高效且安全的长期推理压缩。

Abstract: Long horizon language model agents continually accumulate reasoning history, increasing context length and inference cost even after earlier decisions have been executed and observed. Unlike static Chain of Thought compression, removing historical reasoning can change future actions and the resulting interaction trajectory. We study when such reasoning can be safely forgotten. We propose Interaction Aware Compression for Long Horizon Reasoning (ICLR), a training free online method that ranks reasoning blocks using frozen proxy entropy while preserving actions, tool calls, and observations. On 260 WorkBuddyBench tasks, ICLR improves average reward from 0.699 to 0.718, while reducing input, output, and cache read tokens by 25.5%, 14.4%, and 33.3%, respectively. Ablations reveal trajectory amplification, where local reasoning deletion produces nonlinear changes in total computation by altering subsequent interaction. Representation probing, activation patching, and controlled trajectory analyses further suggest that historical reasoning becomes more replaceable once task relevant derived state has been reliably externalized into code, files, tool outputs, or environmental feedback. These results characterize agent reasoning as dynamic working state rather than permanent interaction history.

</details>


### [90] [Ontology-Mediated Neurosymbolic Constraint Acquisition from Multiple Stakeholders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29876)
*Stefan Bischof,Juliana Kainz,Danilo Valerio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，通过OWL配置本体统一来自LLM助手（软性利益相关者偏好）和硬件规格（硬性物理与工程限制）的异构约束输入，利用描述逻辑检测不一致，并生成可解释的符号化反馈，支持LLM与用户交互式协商。剩余冲突通过基于优先级的松弛机制解决，已在FLEXI项目微电网案例中验证，具有多利益相关方、分布式约束获取场景的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号研究依赖预设的符号规范，未解决需求与约束获取与形式化这一上游挑战。实际应用中，约束来源多样（如人类偏好与物理限制），且权威性不一，亟需一种能整合异构输入并支持动态协商的框架。

Method: 采用OWL配置本体作为中介，融合LLM提取的软性偏好与硬件规格定义的硬性限制；利用描述逻辑进行一致性检查；生成符号解释以支持人机交互式协商；对无法解决的冲突采用优先级驱动的松弛策略。

Result: 在微电网场景下成功识别并处理了多源约束冲突，实现了从非正式需求到可执行符号规范的转化；系统具备可解释性与可交互性，支持动态调整。

Conclusion: 该方法有效填补了神经符号系统中约束获取与形式化的空白，适用于多利益相关方、分布式权威的复杂系统设计场景，为未来智能系统的需求工程提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic research typically assumes a pre-existing symbolic specification, leaving the upstream challenge of acquiring and formalizing requirements and constraints largely unaddressed. We present an architecture that fills this gap by using an OWL configuration ontology to mediate between neural constraint sources and downstream consumers. In this framework, LLM assistants elicit soft stakeholder preferences, while hardware specifications define hard physical and engineering limits. The ontology unifies these heterogeneous inputs, leverages description logic to identify unsatisfiability, and generates symbolic explanations that enable LLMs to interactively renegotiate terms with users. Any remaining conflicts are resolved downstream via priority-based relaxation. We illustrate our approach on a microgrid use case from the FLEXI project and argue its generalizability to multi-stakeholder domains where constraint acquisition is distributed across human and automated sources of unequal authority.

</details>


### [91] [Qwen-Planner-Agent: A Closed-Loop AI-for-AI Framework for Real-World Mobile Planner Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29892)
*Tingyu Qu,Weigao Sun,Yuecheng Liu,Yucheng Zhao,Yi Zhu,Yifeng Ding,Qiyi Wang,Sihan Cao,Pengkun Jiao,Hanlei Xie,Xiongwei Wu,Qichao Wang,Haodong Zhang,Jiajun Liu,Yuhao Wang,Yuqing Xie,Junpeng Zhao,Long Chen,Ming Ma,Sihan Yang,Ziwang Zhao,Yanhao Jia,Liangquan Gong,Feida Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在闭环AI-for-AI框架下构建Qwen-Planner-Agent，通过AI驱动的数据生成、训练优化与模型-工具协同进化，实现对复杂长时序任务（如移动端规划）的高效处理。该框架包含三大模块：AI for Data构建人类参与的智能数据飞轮；AI for Training结合监督冷启动与混合环境在线强化学习，并引入CARE机制降低推理和工具使用成本；AI for Execution则通过执行证据驱动的反馈循环，动态协调记忆、技能与工具，实现模型与工具的持续进化。Qwen-Planner-Agent在MobilePA-Bench上表现最优，在工具使用、记忆、技能及子代理协作方面均超越基线模型，且在非移动场景中也保持良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI是否可同时作为开发对象与主动参与者，推动下一代AI系统的自迭代发展。面对移动规划等复杂长时任务中可靠性挑战与真实设备交互成本高的问题，亟需一种可扩展、可持续改进的闭环AI开发范式。

Method: 提出一个闭环AI-for-AI框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) AI for Data：利用专业化智能体构建人机协同的数据飞轮，实现任务设计、轨迹收集、数据清洗与反馈引导的闭环；(ii) AI for Training：采用监督冷启动+混合环境在线强化学习，引入Competence-Aware Reward-and-Advantage Engineering (CARE)以降低推理与工具调用开销；(iii) AI for Execution：基于执行证据驱动的运行时协调机制，整合记忆、技能与工具，并将结构化反馈与失败记录回传至模型与工具的联合优化。

Result: Qwen-Planner-Agent在MobilePA-Bench上达到最佳综合性能，显著优于基线模型，尤其在工具使用、记忆能力、技能掌握和子代理协作方面提升明显；在非移动类基准测试中亦表现出增强性能，同时保持了良好的通用能力。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了AI在自我开发与迭代中的可行性，所提出的闭环框架有效支持了复杂任务下的可靠性和可扩展性，为未来自主演化的智能系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The rapid progression of large language models is extending AI from passive content generation into the active workflows of engineering and scientific discovery. This shift raises a compelling question: can AI be both the object of development and an active participant in building next-generation AI systems? We explore this question by building Qwen-Planner-Agent within a closed-loop AI-for-AI framework for scalable development and iterative improvement. Mobile planning offers a demanding test of this approach: complex, long-horizon tasks challenge agent reliability, while costly real-device interaction limits development scalability. The framework connects data production, model training, and deployment through a shared action-feedback-verification contract. (i) AI for Data builds a human-gated agentic data flywheel in which specialized agents construct tasks, collect interaction trajectories, curate and balance training data, and use training feedback to guide subsequent data generation. (ii) AI for Training combines a supervised planning cold start with hybrid-environment online agentic reinforcement learning, where we introduce Competence-Aware Reward-and-Advantage Engineering (CARE) to reduce reasoning and tool-use costs while preserving task performance. (iii) AI drives model--harness co-evolution through an execution-evidence-driven loop that orchestrates memory, skills, and tools at runtime and feeds structured action feedback and preserved failure traces back into coordinated model and harness adaptation. Qwen-Planner-Agent achieves the best overall performance among all evaluated models and systems on MobilePA-Bench, improving over its base model across tool use, memory, skills, and sub-agent coordination. Further evaluations of our model show improvements across non-mobile agentic benchmarks while largely preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [92] [Who Holds the Pen? Let Specifications, Not Agents, Sign Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29921)
*Haiqing Li,Xin Ma,Yinhao Wu,Wenliang Zhong,Feng Jiang,Thao M. Dang,Xiao Hu,Hehuan Ma,Yuzhi Guo,Junzhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型代理在执行任务时存在理解-执行差距和状态-权威差距，即代理虽能理解指令但未能正确执行，或声称完成但未达到规定状态。通过在SkillsBench上分析509个基于源的指令，发现各模型仅79.6%–86.4%满足要求，而完成声明率远高于评估通过率。为此，作者提出SpecHarness框架，将规范转化为可追溯的责任，并通过版本化责任状态来管理执行与最终确认，使可验证要求在运行时被中介或验证，模糊或主观要求则作为建议。实验表明，规范不仅是行为指导，更可成为合规执行与完成的权威依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理在执行任务时缺乏独立的规范权威边界，导致理解与执行不一致，以及完成声明与实际状态不符的问题。为解决这一问题，需要建立规范对执行和完成状态的真正控制权。

Method: 提出SpecHarness框架，将可见的规范（如提示、工作区信息、技能说明）转化为带有来源链接的责任项，通过版本化的责任状态来管理执行过程和最终确认，区分可验证与主观/模糊的要求，并在运行时进行验证或中介。

Result: 在指南遵循和生成任务中，采用SpecHarness后，模型的执行与完成状态更加符合规范要求，显著降低虚假完成率，提升了规范的权威性与可验证性，证明了规范不仅能指导行为，还能作为合规性的权威依据。

Conclusion: 通过分离代理提议与权威状态认定，引入可追踪、可验证的规范责任机制，能够有效弥合理解-执行和状态-权威之间的差距，使规范真正成为控制执行与完成的权威标准。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly combine generation, decision-making, execution, and self-evaluation within a single agentic loop. Although they operate under external specifications such as task instructions, guidelines, output schemas, and reusable skills, these specifications typically remain context for the same model that acts and declares completion, leaving no independent specification authority boundary. We identify two resulting gaps. The understanding--execution gap arises when a requirement is understood but not satisfied in execution; the state--authority gap arises when an agent's interpretation or completion claim does not establish the required state. On SkillsBench, using only agent-visible prompts, workspace information, and injected skill specifications, we extract 509 source-grounded task directions. Across seven models, only 79.6%--86.4% are satisfied, while completion-claim rates exceed official evaluator pass rates by 28.7--37.9 percentage points. We therefore separate agent proposals from authoritative state. Agents may plan, act, and request completion, but only admissible evidence from qualified providers may establish specification-governed state. SpecHarness operationalizes this principle by compiling visible specifications into source-linked obligations and governing execution and finalization through versioned obligation state. Verifiable requirements are mediated or validated at runtime, while ambiguous or subjective requirements remain advisory. Experiments on guideline-following and artifact-generation tasks show that specifications can serve not merely as behavioral guidance, but as authority over compliant execution and completion.

</details>


### [93] [Neuro-symbolic AI for Industrial Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29947)
*Danilo Valerio,Philipp Kogler,Stefan Bischof,Thomas Hubauer,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了神经符号人工智能（NeSy）方法在工业级产品配置中的应用，提出三种集成策略：混合推理、混合微调和混合训练，并将其应用于配置助手系统。研究强调了可靠、可解释且可信的AI在工程环境中的重要性，提出了实用的设计选择，并指出了将NeSy方法从学术原型扩展到工业规模的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽在生成任务中表现优异，但其概率特性使其难以满足工业产品配置对语法正确性、语义一致性及可制造性的严格要求。因此需要更可靠的AI方法，如神经符号AI，以确保配置系统的可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 提出并分析三种NeSy集成策略：混合推理、混合微调和混合训练；通过构建工业配置助手验证这些方法的实际应用效果；总结部署可信AI在工程环境中的设计原则。

Result: 成功将NeSy方法应用于工业配置场景，实现语法正确、语义一致且可制造的输出；提炼出一系列适用于工程环境的实用设计决策；揭示了规模化应用面临的核心挑战。

Conclusion: NeSy方法为构建工业级可信配置系统提供了可行路径，但如何实现从小型学术原型到大规模工业系统的有效扩展仍是亟待解决的关键问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive performance on a wide range of generative tasks. Yet their probabilistic nature makes them, in isolation, fundamentally unsuited for industrial product configuration, where outputs must be syntactically valid, semantically consistent with a knowledge base of hundreds of features and rules, and producible by an existing manufacturing chain. We argue that Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI methods lay out a promising path towards industrial-grade configurators that are reliable by design, explainable, and trustworthy. This paper describes a taxonomy of three NeSy integration strategies, namely hybrid inference, hybrid fine-tuning, and hybrid training, exploring their usage in the configuration domain. We report our effort to operationalize NeSy concepts in an industrial configuration copilot and derive a set of practical design choices for deploying trustworthy AI in engineering environments. We close with a discussion of open research challenges we consider most pressing, in particular how to scale NeSy methods from small academic demonstrators to the size of industrial configurators.

</details>


### [94] [ENDOPROMPT: Victim-Side Pseudo-References for Utility Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29948)
*Qingyu Wu,Zeyu Feng,Yongda Yu,Yuzhe Luo,Hua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ENDOPROMPT是一种白盒方法，通过从无标签指令中学习破坏性前缀来降低任务性能。其生成器以请求文本为输入，利用干净的受害者续写作为伪参考，通过局部搜索识别降低续写概率的前缀，并结合偏好拟合与奖励精炼将信号提炼至生成器。部署时，生成器可为每个请求生成一个前缀而无需进一步搜索。在四个指令微调模型和七个良性基准测试的完整数据集上，该方法平均使任务效用下降26.8个百分点，28个测试单元中有27个为负向变化。失败分析显示存在输出扩展和前缀重复问题；控制实验表明请求匹配并未带来显著降级优势。该方法利用受害者监督揭示了任务弱点，无需基准反馈或预设失败响应。代码将在论文接受后公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入攻击多依赖任务标签或预定义目标响应，而真实场景中这些信息可能不可得。本文旨在探索无需依赖外部反馈或特定失败响应的情况下，如何有效发现并利用模型的性能脆弱性，从而实现对任务效用的隐蔽降级。

Method: ENDOPROMPT采用白盒框架，通过生成器从无标签指令中学习破坏性前缀。其核心流程包括：以请求文本为输入，使用干净的受害者续写作为伪参考；通过局部搜索寻找能降低续写概率的前缀；基于同一指令内的比较进行偏好拟合，并通过奖励精炼将降级信号固化到生成器中；部署阶段仅需生成器单次前缀生成，无需额外搜索。

Result: 在四个指令微调模型和七个良性基准测试的完整数据集上，ENDOPROMPT实现了平均26.8个百分点的任务效用下降，28个测试单元中有27个呈现负向变化，表明其具有高度一致性与有效性。失败分析指出存在输出膨胀与前缀复用现象，且控制实验未发现请求匹配带来的降级优势。

Conclusion: ENDOPROMPT展示了仅通过受害者自身输出监督即可有效发现并触发模型任务性能下降的能力，无需依赖预设失败响应或外部基准反馈。该方法为评估大模型在真实场景下的鲁棒性提供了新视角，具备潜在安全研究价值。代码将在论文被接受后开源。

Abstract: Prompt injection can degrade benign task performance without eliciting harmful content. Yet many attack objectives depend on task labels or predefined target responses. We present ENDOPROMPT, a white-box method that learns utility-degrading prefixes from unlabeled instructions. Its generator takes the request text as input. Clean victim continuations serve as pseudo-references: local search identifies prefixes that reduce continuation likelihood, and preference fitting on comparisons within the same instruction, followed by reward refinement, distills this signal into a generator. At deployment, the generator produces one prefix per request without further victim-side search. Across four instruction-tuned models and the complete splits of seven benign benchmarks, ENDOPROMPT yields a mean utility change of -26.8 percentage points; 27 of 28 cells are negative. Failure analysis reveals output expansion and prefix reuse; the controls do not establish a degradation advantage from request matching. Victim-derived supervision can reveal utility weaknesses without benchmark feedback or prescribed failure responses. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [95] [Augur: A Synthetic Decision Lab for Rehearsing Reactions to Product and Policy Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29952)
*Rahul Khedar,Mayank Malhotra,Avinash Karn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Augur通过构建知识图谱、生成用户角色并模拟交互，评估产品或政策变更的公众反应。研究发现，前沿大模型与开源模型之间的性能差距主要源于评估方式不明确，而非实际能力差异。通过三重验证：（1）提示词设计可使同一模型得分从0%提升至73%；（2）仅通过改进提示中的决策分类体系，所有前沿模型性能提升24-34个百分点，且在公平提示下，本地部署的Qwen3-32B LoRA-SFT表现优于甚至等同于所有前沿模型；（3）模型性能提升伴随对教师模型的拟合增强，但真实准确率未同步上升，且整个流程放大了“过度悲观”偏差。此外，合成反应层在盲测中能捕获67%-90%真实公众关切，尤其在决策困难时价值显著。所有数据和代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估产品或政策变更的公众反应是决策前的关键问题。现有方法依赖预训练大模型，但其评估结果受提示设计影响极大，缺乏统一标准，导致难以判断真实模型能力。本文旨在揭示评估偏差的本质，并验证一个可审计、可复现的反应模拟系统（Augur）的有效性。

Method: 构建基于变更文档的类型化知识图谱，生成具有行为特征的虚拟用户角色，模拟公众互动过程，输出包含五类行动建议的决策备忘录。使用Gold-50数据集（50个真实案例，有已知结果）进行评估，对比不同模型与提示设置下的表现。采用配对麦克内马尔检验与霍尔姆校正进行统计分析，并进行盲评与预注册消融实验验证合成反应的真实性与价值。

Result: 1. 提示词设计主导性能差异，同一模型在不同提示下得分波动高达73%；2. 优化提示中的决策分类体系使所有前沿模型性能提升24-34个百分点，且在公平条件下无显著差异；3. 模型对教师的拟合度提高，但真实准确率未提升，系统存在系统性‘过悲观’偏差；4. 合成公众反应在盲测中捕获67%-90%真实关切，尤其在高难度决策中贡献最大；5. 所有实验结果及完整流程代码公开可复现。

Conclusion: 当前大模型评估中观察到的能力差距，很大程度上是由评估设计不规范造成的，而非模型本身能力差异。优化提示工程与评价标准，可显著缩小性能差距。所提出的Augur系统能有效模拟公众反应，具备可审计性和实用性，为政策与产品发布前的风险评估提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Before a product or policy change ships, the question that matters is how people will react to it. Augur rehearses that reaction offline: it builds a typed knowledge graph from the change documents, populates a grounded persona market, simulates the interaction, and returns an auditable decision memo recommending one of five actions. We assemble Gold-50, fifty real product and policy episodes whose real-world outcome is known, adjudicated against the public record, and score the five-way release verdict against it.
  Our central finding is methodological and negative: most of the measured gap between frontier cloud models and open-weight models we fine-tune and serve offline is attributable to an under-specified evaluation, not a difference in capability. We show this three ways. First, the prompt envelope alone can dominate the score: holding weights, cases and scorer fixed, one system -- a LoRA-SFT adapter on Qwen3-32B -- swings from 0% to 73%. Second, in a matched 2x2 ablation, defining the decision taxonomy in the prompt -- with no model change -- lifts every frontier model by +24 to +34pp; under the under-specified prompt, Qwen3-32B LoRA-SFT served offline beats all three frontier models (paired McNemar, Holm-corrected), and once the prompt is fair no significant difference from any of them is detected. Third, agreement with the distillation teacher rises without accuracy following, and the full pipeline amplifies a systematic "over-doom" bias rather than improving the verdict. Separately, we validate the reaction layer on its own terms: blind judges across four model families find the synthetic reaction recovers 67-90% of the concerns the public actually raised, and a pre-registered ablation locates its value -- largest where the decision is hardest, redundant near ceiling. The pipeline that regenerates every number and figure here is available from the authors.

</details>


### [96] [Advancing Model Research in AgentX: Long-Horizon Autonomy for Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30001)
*Shuang Yang,Zijie Zhuang,Changxin Lao,Pengbo Xu,Hanwen Xu,Yusheng Huang,Han Gao,Guanchen Wang,Tianbao Ma,Linxun Chen,Peilin Song,Xuming Wang,Chen Li,Fan Wu,Tao Wang,Zibo Zhao,Xiangyu Wu,An Liu,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Chen Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentX-Model 是下一代模型研究框架，采用双代理架构（研究代理与模型代理），通过沙盒环境结合业务输入与预测任务，实现从实验结果中迭代优化研究方向。该框架围绕复现、跟进、组合和诊断四类行动持续推进工业推荐研究，支持自动化的研究闭环。在生产评估中，636次模型变更实验中有560次AUC优于基线；最新线上A/B测试显示，在获取效率、广告投放、观看时长等指标上均有显著提升，且部分模型参数与计算量减少10%。依赖感知的历史回放基准测试表明，复杂调度策略并未带来一致的效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐研究需要基于已有实验结果动态调整后续研究方向，传统方法缺乏系统性迭代机制，难以高效利用历史成果。为解决此问题，提出可自驱动、可积累知识的研究框架，以提升研究效率与模型性能。

Method: 提出AgentX-Model框架，包含研究代理与模型代理双代理结构：研究代理负责生成独立评审的研究提案，模型代理执行多轮实验并返回代码、度量与未解决问题；二者协同形成研究闭环。研究过程围绕‘复现’‘跟进’‘组合’‘诊断’四类动作展开，支持基于反馈与数据的持续优化。

Result: 在生产环境中，560/636次模型变更实验的AUC优于业务基线；部分实验超越其所有祖先模型。线上A/B测试显示：获取效率提升10–15%，目标人群广告支出提升15–20%，观看时长增加0.3–0.8%；最先进模型节省约10%的FLOPs与参数。依赖感知的历史回放测试表明，现有代理已能有效选择候选，复杂调度无明显优势。

Conclusion: AgentX-Model 有效实现了工业推荐研究的自动化与持续迭代，通过双代理协作与四类行动机制，显著提升了模型性能与研发效率。未来可进一步探索更智能的候选筛选与资源分配策略。

Abstract: Sustaining industrial recommendation research requires using the results of one experiment to decide what to investigate next. We present AgentX-Model, the next generation of AgentX's model research framework, which connects proposal development and model experimentation within sandboxes defined by business inputs and prediction tasks. AgentX-Model adopts a dual-agent architecture comprising a Research Agent and a Model Agent. The Research Agent develops independently reviewed proposals from papers and experimental findings, while the Model Agent conducts multi-round investigations and returns code, measurements, and unresolved questions. Using the returned results, the Research Agent selects a starting implementation and formulates the next research question, allowing subsequent experiments to build on earlier findings. We organize this continuing research around four actions: Reproduce, Follow-up, Composition, and Diagnose. The first three actions drive routine research, while Diagnose acquires the evidence needed to choose a repair, including for issues raised by business feedback and online evaluation, such as prediction bias measured by PCOC. Across the production evaluation, 560 of 636 completed model-changing experiments recorded AUC above their business baselines. As research continued, some experiments recorded AUC above every comparable ancestor in their lineages. The five latest online A/B evaluations across different business settings reported gains including 10-15% in acquisition efficiency, 15-20% in target-segment advertising spend, and 0.3-0.8% in watch time; the watch-time model used approximately 10% fewer FLOPs and parameters. A dependency-aware historical-replay benchmark further evaluates research allocation, with initial results showing no consistent efficiency gain from more complex scheduling when agents already analyze and select concrete candidates.

</details>


### [97] [Synthetic Hospital: An Open, Verifiable, Physician-Validated Longitudinal EHR Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30027)
*Christine Park,Valerie Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Synthetic Hospital is a fully synthetic, fact-grounded longitudinal EHR benchmark built from public medical-education materials, designed to overcome privacy and ground truth limitations in clinical AI research. It contains 1,268 patients and 5,602 encounters with ontological grounding (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) and full provenance. The benchmark mimics real EHR systems with standard APIs and access controls. Physicians could not reliably distinguish synthetic records from real ones (53% accuracy). Even top models achieved only moderate performance (F1=0.73), falling short of human experts (best: 0.89), indicating the benchmark's realism and difficulty.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real electronic health record (EHR) data cannot be openly shared due to privacy and ethical concerns, and lacks verifiable ground truth. This limits the development and evaluation of frontier language models in clinical settings.

Method: Synthetic Hospital was constructed using public medical-education materials without any protected health information. All diagnoses, findings, and temporal relationships are grounded in standard medical ontologies (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) with complete provenance tracing. The system simulates real EHR infrastructure with interoperability APIs, role-based access, and function-calling interfaces.

Result: In a blinded physician review, synthetic records were indistinguishable from real ones at near-chance levels (53%). Among 10 tested models, the best achieved a severity-weighted F1 of 0.73 on reconstructing longitudinal problem lists—comparable to average physicians but significantly below top human performance (0.89). Many clinically relevant findings were missed during chart summarization.

Conclusion: Synthetic Hospital provides a realistic, open, and verifiable benchmark for evaluating clinical AI models. Its high fidelity and difficulty underscore the current limitations of frontier models in understanding complex longitudinal patient data, highlighting the need for more robust clinical reasoning capabilities.

Abstract: Frontier language models are rarely used in clinical workflows because the realistic, longitudinal benchmarks needed to develop them are scarce. Real electronic health record (EHR) data cannot be openly shared due to privacy, ethics or data use issues and it does not contain verifiable ground truth since the chart records only reflect what clinicians documented. We introduce Synthetic Hospital, an open, fully synthetic, fact-grounded longitudinal EHR benchmark that resolves the open sharing and verifiable ground truth barriers. Built entirely from public medical-education material with no protected health information, it comprises 1,268 longitudinal patients and 5,602 encounters, where every diagnosis, finding, and temporal relation is grounded in standard ontologies (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) and with a complete provenance chain back to its source medical education material. Synthetic Hospital is served through a simulated hospital record system that mirrors real EHR infrastructure (standard interoperability APIs, role-based access and function-calling interface). In a blinded review, physicians distinguished its records from real patient charts at near-chance rates (53\%). Across 10 frontier and open models, none approaches ceiling: the best model reconstructs a patient's longitudinal problem list with a severity-weighted F1 of 0.73, level with the mean of seven physicians on a matched subset but well below the best of them (0.89), and misses roughly half of clinically relevant findings when summarizing a chart. Overall, these results highlight that Synthetic Hospital is a difficult and realistic test of clinical AI performance.

</details>


### [98] [How does Adversarial Influence Scale in Multi-Agent Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30028)
*Addison J. Wu,Jasin Cekinmez,Michel Liao,Karthik Narasimhan,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在多智能体协商中，当部分智能体不诚信时，其对整体表现的影响。发现群体中欺骗者所占比例比总人数更关键，欺骗比例上升会导致正确智能体转向错误答案的比率线性增加。与人类在类似从众实验中仅在多数人误导时才受影响不同，大语言模型（LLM）智能体即使在少数欺骗者情况下也容易被误导。此外，诚实智能体的类型显著影响其抗欺骗能力，而欺骗者私下协调反而降低其有效性。因此，单纯增加智能体数量并不能有效防御欺骗行为，因为对手可相应扩大规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多智能体系统中非诚信行为（如欺骗）的影响，尤其是在群体规模扩大时，如何影响整体决策质量，揭示现有协同机制的脆弱性。

Method: 通过模拟多智能体协商场景，控制欺骗者比例和互动模式，对比不同大语言模型作为诚实或欺骗智能体的表现，分析其最终决策偏差与欺骗比例的关系，并考察协调策略对欺骗效果的影响。

Result: 欺骗比例与诚实智能体的错误转换率呈线性关系；即使欺骗者为少数，LLM智能体仍易受骗；诚实智能体的模型类型显著影响其鲁棒性；欺骗者私密协调反而削弱其破坏力。

Conclusion: 增加智能体数量并非抵御欺骗的有效手段，因为欺骗者可随群体规模同步扩张。系统设计需考虑对抗性策略与智能体间的信任机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation can improve performance, but what happens when some agents do not act in good faith? In practice, an agent may be deceptive and work to subvert the group, whether through its own objectives or external instruction. We study how susceptibility to deception scales as groups increase in size and deceivers become more prevalent. It is not the number of agents in the group that matters, but the proportion of deceivers. We observe that the defection rate, how often initially correct agents switch to an incorrect final answer, rises linearly with this proportion. Whereas humans in comparable conformity studies are reliably swayed only when misleading confederates form a majority, LLM agents defect regularly even when deceivers remain a minority. Susceptibility also depends on which models are interacting, especially on the honest agent side. Unexpectedly, allowing deceivers to coordinate privately can make them less effective. Altogether, our results show that adding more agents is therefore not a sufficient defense, because the adversary can simply scale with the group.

</details>


### [99] [Style, Not Self: Surface Cues Explain Zero-Shot Code Attribution by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30048)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Surendrabikram Thapa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在无监督情况下识别自身生成代码的能力，发现模型可能因偏好自身输出而产生偏见，甚至出现模型间串通的风险。实验测试了多个商业模型在MBPP、HumanEval和DS-1000等基准上的表现，结果显示：在单个解决方案判断任务中，平衡准确率仅为49-58%，原始准确率受模型自认作者倾向影响较大；在成对比较任务中，准确率与生成代码长度高度相关（r=0.93），且存在系统性反转现象。通过移除注释、文档字符串、类型提示和局部变量名等元信息后，大多数结果退化至随机水平，仅少数仍可区分，表明模型依赖表面特征而非语义内容进行判断。此外，Claude Haiku的自我偏好也消失。研究建议报告平衡准确率、启发式基线及标签一致性以提升评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否能识别自己生成的代码，以及这种能力可能引发的自洽偏见或模型间串通风险，从而改进代码评估方法的公正性与可信度。

Method: 选取五款主流大模型生成MBPP、HumanEval和DS-1000的解题代码，另七款模型参与MBPP任务；随后让不同模型作为评估者，在四种任务中判断代码归属：从一对代码中选出自己的解法、判断单个代码是否为自己所写、识别指定模型的代码、盲评代码质量。进一步采用规则化方法去除文档字符串、注释、类型提示和局部命名等特征，并重新测试模型表现。

Result: 在单个解决方案任务中，平衡准确率为49-58%，原始准确率范围为38-67%，主要反映模型自认倾向；在成对任务中，准确率与代码长度高度相关（r=0.93），部分组合出现一致反转；经过特征清洗后，12个测试中的10个退化至随机水平，其余两个仍受长度差异影响，但训练分类器仍可区分多数情况；Claude Haiku的自我偏好在清洗后消失。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在识别自身代码时表现出显著偏差，其判断依赖于表面特征而非语义内容，存在自偏和串通风险。建议在评估中报告平衡准确率、提供启发式基线并检验标签一致性，以增强评估的客观性和可重复性。

Abstract: If a language model can recognize code it wrote, it may favor that code as a judge, and instances of one model monitoring each other could collude. We test this zero-shot on current commercial models. Five LLMs generate solutions to MBPP, HumanEval, and DS-1000, seven more to MBPP, and models act as evaluators in four tasks: picking their own solution from a pair, judging whether a single solution is their own, identifying which of two solutions a named model wrote, and judging quality blind. In the single-solution task, balanced accuracy is 49-58% for all 15 model-benchmark combinations, while raw accuracy (38-67%) mostly reflects how readily a model claims authorship. In the pairwise task, accuracy across 14 evaluator-opponent combinations correlates at r=0.93 with how often the evaluator's solution is longer. Attribution to a named model succeeds on some pairs and is consistently inverted on others. A rule-based normalization that strips docstrings, comments, type hints, and local names preserves Pass@1 and leaves ten of twelve re-tested results at chance; the other two follow a length difference it leaves, although a trained classifier still separates most normalized pairs. Claude Haiku's self-preference also disappears. We recommend reporting balanced accuracy, heuristic baselines, and label consistency.

</details>


### [100] [NNV3: Expanding Neural Network Verification to New Architectures and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30050)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Samuel Sasaki,Ben Wooding,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Muhammad Usama Zubair,Navid Hashemi,Hongchao Zhang,Waseem Abbas,Ipek Oguz,Meiyi Ma,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NNV3 是神经网络验证工具的最新版本，扩展了对多种深度学习模型（如FFNN、CNN、RNN、GNN等）的正式验证能力，引入了ModelStar、VolumeStar和GraphStar等新星集形式，支持权重扰动、视频与3D体数据、图神经网络的验证，并结合基于保形推理的概率可达性分析与公平性验证功能。新增多个实际应用场景的基准测试，提供统一文档与教程，显著提升工具的完整性、鲁棒性与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对深度学习模型在安全关键系统中应用日益增长的需求，需提供一套强大、灵活且可扩展的形式化验证框架，以覆盖更广泛的AI模型类型和复杂应用场景，同时兼顾确定性与概率性分析能力，增强工具的实用性与可访问性。

Method: 基于集合传播与星集（Star-set）表示法，构建多类星集结构（ModelStar、VolumeStar、GraphStar）以建模不同输入与网络特性的不确定性；引入保形推理实现概率可达性分析；设计公平性验证模块（FairNNV）用于检测连续输入空间中的反事实与个体公平性问题；集成多样化基准测试与开发者支持资源。

Result: NNV3成功实现了对多种深度学习模型（包括图像、序列、图结构、视频/体数据等）的高效形式化验证；在恶意软件检测、电力系统建模、医学影像、动作识别等真实场景中表现良好；支持从确定性到概率性分析的无缝切换，具备良好的可扩展性与用户友好性。

Conclusion: NNV3标志着神经网络验证工具向全面化、实用化和易用化方向的重要进展，为各类学习型嵌入式系统提供了强有力的形式化保障，是迈向可信人工智能的关键一步。

Abstract: We present NNV3, the latest version of the Neural Network Verification (NNV) tool, a MATLAB framework for formal verification of deep learning models and learning-enabled cyber-physical systems. Building on the set-based reachability foundation of NNV 1.0 (FFNNs, CNNs, NNCS) and NNV 2.0 (RNNs, SSNNs, neural ODEs), NNV3 introduces new members of the Star-set family: ModelStar for verifying networks under weight perturbation, VolumeStar for video and 3D volumetric inputs, and GraphStar for graph neural networks. A conformal-inference-based probabilistic reachability mode complements sound analysis for problems where deterministic verification is intractable, while FairNNV certifies counterfactual and individual fairness properties over continuous input regions. NNV3 introduces new benchmarks for malware detection, graph-based power-system models, medical imaging, variable-length time series data, and action recognition. NNV3 also incorporates tutorials and developer guides through a unified documentation site. This paper details these major updates, demonstrating NNV's maturation into a comprehensive, robust, and accessible verification tool for a diverse range of AI systems.

</details>


### [101] [SciWalker: Synthesizing Scientific Coding Problems with Operator Graphs and Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30054)
*Chenxi Li,Wenxuan Zeng,Yun Luo,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Yu Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciWalker 是一个用于合成科学编程问题的框架，通过操作符链采样和执行反馈生成高质量、可执行的科学编程任务。该框架结合科学库接口与操作模式构建操作符图，采样操作链作为计算工作流提示，利用大语言模型生成问题描述、参考解法和测试用例，并通过执行反馈迭代修复失败生成。最终构建了涵盖5个科学领域和32个子领域的8,178个高质量问题。在Qwen3.5-9B上使用GSPO算法进行强化学习训练后，科学代码子问题准确率提升9.9个百分点（从29.3%到39.2%），并在代码生成、修复和推理任务中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在科学编程能力方面受限于高质量训练数据的稀缺性，手动编写真实问题成本高且难以覆盖多样化的科学领域与算法组合，因此需要一种高效、可扩展的方法来生成兼具科学性、计算多样性和可执行性的编程问题。

Method: SciWalker 采用操作符链采样与执行反馈机制：首先基于科学库接口和操作模式构建操作符图，然后采样形成操作链作为计算工作流提示；在此基础上，利用大语言模型生成问题陈述、参考解法和测试用例；对于生成失败的情况，通过执行反馈进行迭代修复；整个过程结合结构化工作流构建、验证与质量审查，实现规模化、高质量的问题生成。

Result: 成功构建了8,178个高质量科学编程问题，覆盖5个科学领域和32个子领域；在Qwen3.5-9B上应用GSPO算法进行强化学习训练后，科学代码子问题准确率从29.3%提升至39.2%，增幅达9.9个百分点，并在代码生成、修复及推理等多个基准测试中均表现提升。

Conclusion: SciWalker 有效解决了高质量科学编程训练数据稀缺的问题，通过结构化工作流与执行反馈相结合的方式，实现了可扩展、科学性强、可执行的编程任务生成，显著提升了大语言模型在科学编程方面的性能，为后续研究提供了可复现的数据集与方法框架。

Abstract: Improving the scientific coding capabilities of large language models (LLMs) requires high-quality training data. However, such data remain scarce because manually authoring realistic problems is costly and time-consuming, while systematically covering diverse scientific domains and algorithmic combinations remains challenging. To address this, we introduce SciWalker, a framework for synthesizing scientific coding problems through operator-chain sampling and execution feedback. The framework combines scientific library interfaces with operation modes to instantiate operators, organizes them into operator graphs, and samples operator chains as computational workflow cues. Guided by these cues, we adopt LLMs to generate scientifically grounded problem statements, reference solutions, and tests, with failed generations iteratively repaired using execution feedback. By combining structured workflow composition with verification and quality review, SciWalker enables scalable task generation while promoting scientific grounding, computational diversity, and executability. Using this framework, we construct 8,178 high-quality problems spanning 5 scientific domains and 32 subdomains. To evaluate their training utility, we conduct reinforcement learning on Qwen3.5-9B using the GSPO algorithm. This training improves SciCode subproblem accuracy by 9.9 percentage points, from 29.3% to 39.2%, with gains across scientific code generation, code repair, and reasoning benchmarks. The code for SciWalker is available at https://github.com/lichenx1/SciWalker.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30063)
*Aditya Cowsik,Kfir Dolev,Michael Y. Li,G. Bruno De Luca,Nourya Cohen,Noah D. Goodman,Yoav Levine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为自对弈预训练零数据（Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data）的新方法，旨在让模型自主生成对其自身改进最有用的合成数据，从而摆脱对人工标注数据的依赖。该方法基于可计算结构的搜索空间，利用通用图灵机生成字节序列，通过生成器与学习器的协同训练实现自适应课程学习。生成器通过强化学习优化，以产生学习器能力边界上的数据；学习器则进行标准交叉熵训练。实验表明，该方法在无自然数据训练的前提下，零样本性能随计算资源增加呈现可预测的缩放规律，并展现出上下文学习能力及发现数学序列的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的预训练依赖于大量人工筛选的数据，限制了数据规模和多样性。为突破这一瓶颈，研究希望让模型自主生成最有利于自身提升的训练数据，实现真正意义上的无限数据来源，仅受算力限制。

Method: 提出自对弈预训练框架，其中两个模型协同工作：生成器设计由通用图灵机解释的程序，生成字节序列；学习器则对这些序列进行自回归预测。生成器使用强化学习策略，目标是生成位于学习器能力前沿的数据，形成动态适应性课程。整个过程不依赖任何自然数据。

Result: 在多个自然数据集上，零样本损失随着计算量的增加表现出可预测的缩放趋势，验证了该方法的有效性。模型还展现出上下文学习能力，并在训练过程中自发发现如斐波那契数列等数学模式。

Conclusion: 该研究首次展示了模型可通过自对弈机制自主生成有效训练数据的可行性，为实现无需人类干预的无限数据预训练提供了概念验证，具有深远潜力。

Abstract: Advances in language modeling have been driven by scaling pretraining on ever more data. Yet, the training data is still largely curated on the model's behalf. A more general approach to pretraining would let the model learn to generate the data most useful for its own improvement. This would provide an effectively unbounded source of training data, limited by compute rather than human knowledge. We introduce Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data, an initial proof-of-concept towards realizing this vision. Our procedure casts synthetic data generation as a search over the space of all computable structure, taking inspiration from Solomonoff induction. Starting from random initialization, two models learn in tandem: a generator proposes programs interpreted by a universal Turing machine, generating byte sequences, while a learner autoregressively predicts these byte sequences. The learner is trained with standard cross-entropy, while the generator is trained with reinforcement learning to produce sequences at the frontier of the learner's capabilities, yielding an adaptive curriculum. A universal Turing machine gives us a search space over all computable data-generating processes, imposing little domain-specific structure, and self-play searches over this space for useful training data. We test whether zero-shot performance on natural data improves predictably with self-play compute; this is a clean test of transfer since neither generator nor learner is trained on natural data. Across several natural datasets, zero-shot loss exhibits predictable scaling in compute. The models also exhibit in-context learning, and discover recognizable mathematical sequences during training.

</details>


### [103] [PrivDrift: Auditing User-Secret Leakage Under Topic Drift in Active LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30094)
*Luciano Maldonado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrivDrift 是一个用于审计用户在对话中披露的敏感信息是否在话题转移和说服性探查后仍可被恢复的基准。该基准包含1000个受控多轮对话，包含种子秘密、内容密集的话题漂移回合和标准化提取探测。在三个具有长上下文窗口的大型语言模型上，对话级别的混合泄露率仍然很高，范围为38.7%至54.6%，且受模型、秘密类型和说服强度的影响显著。在测试的漂移窗口内，额外的话题漂移并不能可靠降低泄露，表明在活跃的LLM环境中，隐私风险应被视为一种持续的行为失效模式，而不仅仅是训练数据记忆或即时越狱行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当用户在对话中披露敏感信息时，这些信息可能在对话转向无关话题后仍可通过后续提示被恢复，因此需要评估大语言模型在长期交互中的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 构建了包含1000个受控多轮对话的PrivDrift基准，包含种子秘密、内容密集的话题漂移回合和标准化提取探测，用于评估不同模型在对话漂移后的秘密恢复能力。

Result: 在三个具有长上下文窗口的大语言模型中，对话级别的混合泄露率高达38.7%至54.6%，且不受额外话题漂移的显著影响，表明隐私风险是持续性的行为问题。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在活跃对话环境中的隐私风险应被视为一种持久的行为失效模式，而非仅限于训练数据记忆或即时越狱行为，需重新评估其隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate as persistent assistants in user-facing, shared-session, and tool-augmented settings. When users disclose sensitive information during an active conversation, that information may remain behaviorally recoverable through later prompts even after the dialogue shifts to unrelated topics. We introduce \textbf{PrivDrift}, a benchmark for auditing whether user-disclosed secrets remain recoverable after conversational topic drift and persuasion-based probing. PrivDrift contains 1{,}000 controlled multi-turn dialogues with seeded secrets, content-dense drift turns, and standardized extraction probes. Across three LLMs with extended context windows, dialogue-level hybrid leakage remains substantial, ranging from 38.7\% to 54.6\%, and varies strongly by model, secret type, and persuasion intensity. Within the tested drift window, additional topic drift does not reliably reduce leakage, suggesting that privacy risk in active LLM contexts should be evaluated as a persistent behavioral failure mode rather than only as training-data memorization or immediate jailbreak behavior.

</details>


### [104] [HEXIS: Compiling Skills into Extended Finite State Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30123)
*Minghao LI*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HEXIS将代理技能编译为扩展有限状态机，分离知识与控制流，通过局部指令指导推理与生成，明确的转移条件决定后续操作。其增量编译器映射技能条款与工具接口，对齐开发轨迹以识别缺失操作和依赖关系，并通过添加或重用状态来更新系统，确保静态检查和历史轨迹回放验证。在四个基准测试中，相较于Skill + ReAct，HEXIS平均提升成功率16.1个百分点；Qwen3.8-27B模型在各基准上减少38.4%-88.9%的执行令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理技能在应用时需反复推断如何使用及下一步操作，导致任务推理与控制决策耦合，易出现步骤遗漏或误用。为解决此问题，需分离知识与控制流程，提高技能应用的准确性和可复用性。

Method: 提出HEXIS框架，将技能编译为扩展有限状态机；利用局部指令引导状态内的推理与生成；通过状态记录执行进度与中间结果，由显式转移条件决定后续操作；采用增量编译器映射技能条款与工具接口，对齐开发轨迹以发现缺失操作与依赖，动态更新状态与连接，并通过静态检查与历史轨迹回放验证更新有效性。

Result: 在四个基准测试和四种执行器上，HEXIS相比Skill + ReAct平均提升成功率16.1个百分点；Qwen3.8-27B模型在各基准上减少38.4%-88.9%的执行令牌消耗。

Conclusion: HEXIS通过将技能知识与控制流解耦，实现更高效、可靠的任务执行，显著提升成功率并大幅降低计算开销，为复杂任务自动化提供了可扩展、可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable knowledge and instructions, yet agents must repeatedly infer how to apply them and which operation should follow. This couples task reasoning with control decisions, allowing prescribed steps to be omitted or applied incorrectly. We introduce HEXIS, which compiles agent skills into extended finite state machines that separate knowledge from control flow. Skill knowledge is incorporated into local instructions that guide reasoning and generation within states. The machine records execution progress and intermediate results, while explicit transition conditions determine subsequent operations. Our incremental compiler first maps skill clauses and tool interfaces to state operations, local instructions, data bindings, and transitions. It then aligns development traces with existing states to identify missing operations and dependencies. These are incorporated by adding or reusing states and refining their connections. Updates are accepted only after static checks and replay of the current and all previously accepted traces. Across four benchmarks and four executors, HEXIS improves success over Skill + ReAct by 16.1 percentage points on average. Qwen3.8-27B reduces execution tokens by 38.4-88.9% across benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [Screen Before You Serve: Simulation for Production Customer Experience AI Agents at 140M Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30137)
*Edesio Alcoba,Kevin Rossell,Aman Gupta,Shao Tang,Jiwoo Hong,Pabel Carrillo-Mendoza,Wanderson Conceição Ferreira,Alvaro Tedeschi,Zayd Simjee,Shreya Rajpal,Bruno Finardi Hime,Christian Sousa,Luis Moneda,Herbert Fei,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于假设的仿真工作流，用于在部署前筛选客户体验（CX）代理。通过合成客户与代理响应互动，并模拟工具输出，实现无需调用生产后端的多步代理工作流。在Nubank的卡配送和卡管理代理上验证，仿真与生产版本的评估得分高度相关。仿真指导的迭代使交易型净推荐值（tNPS）在实际A/B测试中提升36.69分；同时，在超过16,000次仿真对话中筛选开放权重模型配置，选定模型在后续测试中使自助服务率（SSR）提升8.82个百分点至历史最高水平，且tNPS无显著变化。仿真使大规模探索模型、推理设置和提示成为可能，避免了客户暴露风险，推动了原本难以通过真实实验实现的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管行业，改进客户体验代理面临挑战：需准确识别意图、遵守复杂运营政策并可靠使用工具。传统手动端到端测试覆盖有限，而真实环境实验可能损害客户信任。因此需要一种安全、高效的方法来评估和优化代理。

Method: 提出一种假设驱动的仿真工作流，利用合成客户对代理响应作出反应，结合模拟工具输出，支持多步骤代理任务而不依赖生产后端。通过Snowglobe仿真器在Nubank的卡配送和卡管理代理上进行验证，对比仿真与生产环境的评估结果，并基于仿真结果指导模型迭代与配置筛选。

Result: 仿真与生产版本的二元评估得分高度相关；仿真指导的迭代使tNPS提升36.69分；在16,000+次仿真中筛选出最优模型配置，实测使自助服务率提升8.82个百分点至历史最高，且未影响tNPS。

Conclusion: 仿真工作流使大规模模型、提示和推理策略的探索成为可能，避免客户暴露风险，实现了在真实实验中难以达成的生产优化，为高价值客户服务代理的持续改进提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Customer experience (CX) agents use tools and large language models to address customer requests and guide conversational interactions with an organization's products. Improving these agents, especially in regulated industries, is difficult: they must detect intent, follow complex operational policies and use tools reliably. Manual end-to-end testing offers limited coverage, while live experiments expose customers to failures that can erode trust.
  We present a hypothesis-driven simulation workflow for screening candidate CX agents before deployment. Synthetic customers react to agent responses and simulated tool outputs enable multi-step agentic workflows without invoking production backends. We use the Snowglobe simulator on Nubank's Card Delivery agent and its expanded successor, Card Management - Nubank's highest-volume chat-support agent in Brazil. Across 4 deployed versions, simulated and production version-level binary evaluator scores show high correlation. Simulation-guided iteration increased transactional net promoter score (tNPS) by 36.69 points in a live A/B test. We also screened open-weight configurations in over 16,000 simulated conversations. In a subsequent live A/B test, the selected model increased self-service rate (SSR) by 8.82 percentage points to the highest level observed at Nubank, with no statistically significant change in tNPS. Simulation made broad exploration of models, reasoning settings, and prompts feasible without customer exposure, enabling production improvements that would have been impractical to pursue through live experimentation alone.

</details>


### [106] [EnigmaForge: The Question Is Hidden in the Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30144)
*Daniel Eisner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnigmaForge 是一个全新的基准测试，它不向模型提出问题，而是提供一堆旧文档（如信件、收据、日志等），要求模型从中推理出一个唯一解的逻辑谜题。所有实例由生成而非收集得到，确保数据持续更新。主要评估指标是‘直觉’——即仅凭故事内容完成任务的能力；次要指标是世界重建能力。25个前沿模型在600多个实例上进行了测试，共产生17,400条评分记录。结果表明，直觉能力差异巨大（22倍差距），而事实恢复能力仅差1.6倍，第二好的事实恢复模型在直觉排名中垫底。部分模型因内容过滤器被阻断，说明现有基准可能无意中在衡量过滤行为而非推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试依赖于直接提问，但忽略了模型在无提示下从复杂文本中自主发现和解决隐含逻辑问题的能力。为更真实地评估模型的深层推理与直觉，需要一种无需明确问题、依赖上下文推断的评测方式。

Method: 通过生成包含隐藏逻辑谜题的虚构文档集合（如信件、收据、日志等），利用SAT求解器验证每个谜题的唯一解性，并确保每条线索都对解答具有决定性作用（通过消融证书证明）。测试中，模型仅接收文档，不获问题提示，评估其在无引导下的任务完成率与世界重建能力。

Result: 在25个模型、600+实例的测试中，直觉表现差异显著（22倍差距），远超事实恢复能力（1.6倍差距）；第二好的事实恢复模型在直觉排名中位列第十四；有模型在未被告知问题时表现更好，甚至有一模型对是否告知问题无差别；部分模型因内容过滤器被提前拦截，表明当前基准可能误测了过滤行为。

Conclusion: EnigmaForge 提供了一种更接近人类推理本质的评测范式，强调在缺乏明确指令的情况下从复杂文本中提取隐含信息的能力。它揭示了当前模型在‘直觉推理’方面的巨大差距，并暴露了现有评测体系可能被内容过滤机制扭曲的问题。

Abstract: Most benchmarks hand the model a question. EnigmaForge hands it a stack of old documents and no question at all. Buried in the letters, receipts, and logbook margins is a small logic puzzle whose solution is unique - proved by a SAT solver at generation time, with an ablation certificate showing every clue is load-bearing. Because instances are generated rather than collected, the corpus renews forever. The headline measure is intuition: task success when handed only the story, with world reconstruction as the secondary axis. Twenty-five frontier models ran over 600 instances (17,400 scored records) under three matched conditions. Intuition reshuffles the leaderboard: a 22x spread where fact recovery spans 1.6x, the second-best fact-recoverer ranks fourteenth, one model is indifferent to being told the question, and another is significantly better without it. Several models were blocked by their own content filters before reaching the puzzle - any benchmark scoring refusals as failure is quietly measuring filter behavior.

</details>


### [107] [GRASP: Generating, Revising, and Assessing for Strategic Planning with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30147)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRASP 是一种策略感知的多阶段规划框架，通过将规划流程分解为独立的模块（生成全局指导、探索局部策略、评估轨迹），显著提升复杂任务下的自然语言可执行计划质量。在多个数据集上，GRASP 均达到新基准，相比直接使用 LLM 规划器，准确率提升显著；尤其在多任务场景下，有效缓解性能下降问题，表现优于前沿推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在复杂任务上可靠性下降，现有方法难以生成高质量、可执行的自然语言计划，尤其是在多任务环境下性能急剧恶化。因此需要一种更鲁棒、分阶段、模块化的规划框架来应对复杂性与多任务挑战。

Method: GRASP 采用三阶段设计：(1) GenPlan 预编译全局宏观指导；(2) RevPlan 在隔离上下文窗口中探索多种局部策略；(3) VerPlan 使用多准则判别器独立评估路径。各模块保持上下文隔离并引入宏正则化，增强规划稳定性与可解释性。

Result: GRASP 在 Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling 上提升约 12.4%，ZebraLogic 提升约 30.8%，SciBench Math 也有显著增益；在多任务和交错双任务环境中，相比直接 LLM 规划器，准确率最高提升 16.7%；且超越 GPT-5-mini 等前沿模型达 14.5%。

Conclusion: GRASP 通过模块化、策略感知与上下文隔离的设计，在复杂任务与多任务场景下均实现显著性能提升，为构建高可靠性的自动规划系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) typically exhibit a performance profile where reliability degrades as task complexity increases. We address the challenge of generating high-quality natural language executable plans for complex tasks by introducing $\textbf{GRASP}$, a strategy-aware, multi-stage planning framework. GRASP decouples the planning pipeline across specialized, context-isolated modules: it pre-compiles global macro-guidelines (GenPlan), explores alternative localized strategies within isolated context windows (RevPlan), and independently evaluates trajectories using a multi-criteria discriminator (VerPlan). Empirical evaluations show that GRASP consistently establishes a new state-of-the-art frontier across diverse datasets, yielding substantial accuracy gains over direct LLM planners on Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling ($\sim$12.4$\%$$\uparrow$), ZebraLogic ($\sim$30.8$\%$$\uparrow$), and SciBench Math. Crucially, under multi-task scaling-where standard planners suffer immediate performance collapse-GRASP completely flattens the multi-task degradation penalty. In interleaved dual-task environments, GRASP achieves an absolute accuracy gain of up to 16.7$\%$ over direct LLM planners. Furthermore, by isolating context and enforcing strict macro-regularization, GRASP outperforms frontier reasoning models (such as GPT-5-mini) by a margin of 14.5$\%$.

</details>


### [108] [Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30177)
*Nayoung Choi,Shengjian Chen,Xiaokai Wei,Wenzheng Zhang,Daiyao Yi,Rachit Pareek,Vincent Su,Michelle Gong,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“先蒸馏后强化学习”的搜索感知强化学习框架，用于查询理解任务。首先通过教师-学生监督微调获得结构良好的初始策略，随后在强化学习阶段针对每个查询理解组件设计特定奖励信号，以优化其与搜索引擎的交互效果。实验表明，该方法显著提升了各组件的实用性及下游搜索质量，相比监督微调策略，NDCG@20 提升 8.9 点，相比单一端到端奖励训练提升 3.5 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询理解模型依赖静态标签监督，无法捕捉各组件在真实搜索流水线中的实际影响，导致优化目标与实际性能脱节。需要一种能反映组件级交互效果的优化机制。

Method: 采用‘先蒸馏后强化学习’的两阶段框架：第一阶段通过教师-学生监督微调生成符合格式的初始策略；第二阶段引入搜索感知的强化学习，为每个查询理解组件设计独立的奖励函数，基于其在真实搜索系统中的作用进行优化。

Result: 在 Roblox 搜索数据集上的实验显示，该方法显著提升了各组件的实用性与整体搜索性能，相较于监督微调策略，NDCG@20 提升 8.9 点；相较于使用单一端到端奖励的训练方式，提升 3.5 点。

Conclusion: 提出的搜索感知强化学习框架通过组件级奖励设计，有效解决了传统监督学习中目标与实际系统表现不一致的问题，显著提升了查询理解模块的实用性和下游搜索质量。

Abstract: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.

</details>


### [109] [Jev-Mobile: Jev as an Executor for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30186)
*Linghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev-Mobile proposes a decoupled approach for mobile GUI agents by using VLMs only for high-level planning and lightweight models for frequent action execution, reducing latency and cost while maintaining strong task performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing mobile GUI agents rely heavily on vision-language models (VLMs) at every interaction step, causing high latency and significant model-serving costs. The need to reduce these costs without sacrificing adaptability and performance drives the motivation.

Method: Jev-Mobile employs a two-tiered design: a VLM performs infrequent high-level planning to set local goals, while an accessibility tree defines a structured action space. A fast typed decision model (Jev) then executes multiple actions within this space based on the VLM's plan, minimizing repeated VLM inference.

Result: On AndroidWorld, Jev-Mobile achieves 79% task success—slightly below the Step-wise VLM baseline (84%) but comparable to SeeAct-V (78%). However, it reduces mean end-to-end execution time by 32.7% and model API cost by 73.4% compared to the Step-wise VLM, demonstrating significant efficiency gains.

Conclusion: Decoupling high-level reasoning from low-level action execution via Jev-Mobile effectively improves the efficiency of mobile GUI agents with minimal performance trade-off, offering a practical path toward scalable and cost-effective autonomous agents.

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a common foundation for autonomous mobile GUI agents, but most existing systems rely on the VLM for both planning and action grounding at nearly every interaction step, leading to substantial latency and model-serving cost. We introduce Jev-Mobile, which shifts this paradigm to low-frequency VLM planning and high-frequency lightweight execution: the VLM specifies local goals, the accessibility tree defines a structured executable action space, and Jev, a fast typed decision model, repeatedly selects actions within this space. This design allows multiple GUI actions to be executed under a single VLM decision, reducing expensive VLM inference while preserving adaptive interaction. On the full AndroidWorld task suite, Jev-Mobile achieves 79% task success, compared with 78% for SeeAct-V and 84% for a Step-wise VLM baseline. Among successful trajectories, it reduces mean end-to-end execution time by 32.7% and mean model API cost by 73.4% relative to Step-wise VLM. These results show that decoupling high-level VLM reasoning from low-level action execution can substantially improve mobile GUI agent efficiency while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [110] [SAGE: Mitigating Long-Horizon Reasoning Biases via Topological Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30192)
*Xinyue Zeng,Jiawei Zhang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对大语言模型在稀疏奖励环境下长时程推理的脆弱性问题，提出由复杂推理空间引发的探索偏差和累积偏差是主要原因。为此，作者引入符号闭包分析（SCA）作为理论框架，并设计SAGE框架，通过代数稀疏化与双曲结构引导双重机制，分别缓解探索偏差和累积偏差。在12个基准测试和7种模型家族上，SAGE显著优于现有基线，尤其在安德鲁斯-柯蒂斯问题上提升达8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程推理在稀疏奖励场景下表现不稳定，源于复杂推理空间带来的探索偏差和累积偏差，亟需理论分析与结构化引导方法以提升模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出符号闭包分析（SCA）作为理论工具，构建SAGE框架，结合代数稀疏化（投影至算子索引代数子空间）与双曲结构引导（将推理状态嵌入负曲率空间），实现对推理路径的结构性约束与信号增强。

Result: 在12个基准测试和7种模型家族中，SAGE均显著超越基线；尤其在安德鲁斯-柯蒂斯问题上实现最高8倍性能提升，验证了其在真实长时程任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: SCA为理解长时程推理中的偏差提供理论视角，SAGE通过结构化引导有效缓解探索与累积偏差，在多种任务与模型上展现出强大泛化能力，为非形式化推理任务的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models (LLMs) under sparse-reward regimes. We argue that this brittleness arises from two biases induced by complex reasoning spaces: an exploration bias, where models are drawn toward locally plausible but structurally unstable branches, and a compounding bias, where small local deviations accumulate across depth and suppress rare rewards. We introduce Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA) as a theoretical lens characterizing how branching structures and sparse rewards induce these biases in long-horizon reasoning with local admissibility, and as a design principle for structural priors in less formal reasoning tasks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose SAGE (Structural Admissibility-Guided Exploration), a unified framework that injects structural guidance to alleviate exploration bias and compounding bias in long-horizon reasoning. SAGE combines two complementary structural guidance: algebraic sparsification, which projects locally admissible candidates onto operator-indexed algebraic subspaces to suppress spurious branching and mitigate exploration bias, and hyperbolic structural guidance, which embeds reasoning states into a negatively curved space to provide dense depth-wise signals and mitigate compounding bias. Across 12 benchmarks and 7 model families, SAGE outperforms competitive baselines. In particular, SAGE achieves up to an 8-fold improvement on the Andrews-Curtis problem, an open real-world long-horizon task. Code is available at: https://github.com/Susan571/SAGE-NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [111] [ExplorationBench: Measuring AI Systems' Exploration in Verifiable Alien Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30199)
*Ming Zhang,Zhenghao Xiang,Peizhong Gao,Yujiong Shen,Yuhui Wang,Zhonghan Yue,Shihan Dou,Zhangyue Yin,Junjie Ye,Shichun Liu,Weihuang Zheng,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Hongzhang Liu,Jiaqi Shao,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExplorationBench，一个用于评估AI系统在未知环境中进行科学探索能力的基准框架。该框架通过可验证的‘外星世界’（Alien Worlds）设定，确保规则可执行、与常识冲突，从而防止模型仅靠记忆预训练数据中的知识来解题。包含两个沙箱：AlienCode（31个发现目标，70个任务）和AlienLogic（24个发现目标，70个任务），提供错误手册、环境反馈和专用工具调用模式，支持系统自主探索与迭代。实验评估了10个AI系统，发现最强系统能学习并应用新规则，但表现随探索路径变化大，持续探索可能导致进展停滞甚至倒退。该工作推动了具备真正探索与知识获取能力的AI系统的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估AI在未知环境中进行科学探索的能力面临两大挑战：一是如何验证新假设的真实性，二是如何区分探索发现与预训练知识的简单回忆。现有方法难以有效区分真正的探索行为与知识复现。

Method: 提出ExplorationBench框架，构建基于可执行规则的‘外星世界’环境，利用与人类常识相冲突的规则设计任务，防止模型依赖记忆；通过提供错误手册、任务特定环境反馈和工具调用接口，引导系统自主探索，并在未见任务上评估其发现与应用新知识的能力。

Result: 10个被测AI系统中，最强系统能够学习并应用陌生规则，但不同探索路径间性能差异显著，且持续探索可能引发性能下降或倒退。这表明当前系统仍缺乏稳定的探索-学习机制。

Conclusion: ExplorationBench为评估AI在未知环境中的科学探索能力提供了可验证、可操作的框架，标志着向具备真实知识获取与探索能力的AI系统迈出关键一步。

Abstract: Scientific discovery begins where known problems end. There, AI systems must engage in exploration: framing hypotheses, designing experiments, and iterating on the results. However, evaluating this ability is difficult: (1) how to verify whether a genuinely new hypothesis holds, and (2) how to determine whether a system has discovered it through exploration or merely recalled related knowledge from pre-training data. To this end, we introduce ExplorationBench, which turns the wicked problem of evaluating scientific exploration into a concrete and tractable framework built on verifiable Alien Worlds: their rules are executable, so every answer can be checked exactly, and they conflict with familiar knowledge, so recall alone cannot solve the tasks. The benchmark contains two sandboxes, AlienCode (31 discovery targets, 70 tasks) and AlienLogic (24 discovery targets, 70 tasks). Each sandbox provides a flawed manual, task-specific environmental feedback, and a dedicated tool-call schema. Systems use these resources to explore the sandbox, then solve held-out tasks. We evaluate 10 AI systems and find that the strongest systems can acquire and apply unfamiliar rules, while performance varies substantially across trajectories and continued exploration can stall or reverse earlier gains. ExplorationBench represents a step towards AI systems that can acquire and apply genuinely new knowledge through exploration in unknown environments.

</details>


### [112] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的框架，自动从纵向电子健康记录（EHR）笔记中生成问答对，构建了可持续维护的BRIE基准数据集。该框架经19名临床医生验证，支持动态更新、多答案生成及复杂信息合成评估，揭示当前主流大语言模型在临床信息检索中仍存在重要信息遗漏问题，尤其在跨文档与多就诊记录整合方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床大语言模型评估基准依赖人工构建，成本高、难以更新，且易因技术快速演进而过时，亟需一种可自动、持续生成高质量评估数据的方法以确保模型安全性与实用性。

Method: 基于纵向EHR文本，设计自动化问答对生成框架；通过19名临床医生验证生成质量，构建BRIE基准；支持多答案生成与动态内容刷新，实现对多种大语言模型和推理策略的持续评估。

Result: 在九个大语言模型和五种推理策略下，先进系统频繁遗漏关键临床信息，尤其在需要跨多个文档和就诊记录进行信息综合的问题上表现不佳；BRIE支持更真实、更全面的性能评估，具备抵御模型泄露的能力。

Conclusion: 可扩展的基准生成方法使临床大语言模型能够在快速变化的医疗环境中实现持续、严谨的评估，推动其安全落地应用。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


### [113] [AD-WM: Action-Discriminative World Models for Counterfactual Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30264)
*Jiabin Qiu,Zixuan Chen,Hongye Cao,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AD-WM 是一种用于反事实模型预测控制（MPC）的动作区分联合嵌入世界模型，通过残差潜在动态和预测级别动作恢复正则化，结合逆动力学与归一化恢复目标，以保留动作信息。该模型在多个仿真环境中显著提升规划成功率，尤其在硬启动任务中从3.7%提升至52.0%，并在零样本迁移至真实机器人（Franka）时将抓取成功率从42.2%提升至71.1%。结果表明，世界模型应注重保留动作相关的差异性信息，而不仅优化事实预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜在世界模型侧重于准确预测真实状态转移，但在模型预测控制（MPC）中需要区分同一状态下不同动作的反事实后果。现有模型虽具备低事实预测误差，却无法有效区分候选动作，导致规划性能受限。因此，亟需一种能保留动作相关差异的世界模型，以支持更优的反事实选择。

Method: 提出AD-WM模型，结合残差潜在动态架构与预测层级的动作恢复正则化。引入逆动力学任务与基于条件互信息的归一化恢复目标，促使模型在学习状态转移时保留动作信息；辅助头在测试时被移除，不影响原有MPC流程。

Result: 在OGBench-Cube上，硬启动成功率达52.0%（相比基线3.7%），在五个仿真环境中的四个中平均成功率优于基线。规划诊断显示，事实预测误差与动作排序不匹配闭环成功顺序，而CEM对齐的精英遗憾更贴近实际成功表现。在真实机器人（Franka）上实现零样本迁移，抓取成功率从42.2%提升至71.1%，无需本地适配。

Conclusion: 世界模型在规划任务中不应仅追求事实预测准确性，而应重点保留动作依赖的状态差异信息，以支持有效的反事实决策。AD-WM通过动作区分性建模显著提升了规划性能，适用于复杂任务与跨域迁移。

Abstract: Latent world models are typically trained to predict factual transitions, whereas model predictive control (MPC) must compare alternative actions from the same state. A model can therefore achieve low factual prediction error yet poorly distinguish candidate actions. We introduce AD-WM, an action-discriminative joint-embedding world model for counterfactual MPC. AD-WM combines residual latent dynamics with predictor-level action-recovery regularization, using inverse dynamics and a normalized recovery objective motivated by conditional mutual information. Both objectives encourage planning transitions to preserve action information; their auxiliary heads are discarded at test time, leaving MPC unchanged. On OGBench-Cube, AD-WM improves hard-start success from 3.7% to 52.0% over a matched LeWM baseline and improves mean success over the reproduced baseline in four of five simulation environments. Planning diagnostics show that factual prediction error and whole-bank action ranking do not follow the closed-loop success ordering, whereas CEM-aligned elite regret tracks success more closely. With a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder and matched DROID post-training, AD-WM also improves zero-shot transfer to our Franka setup, increasing basic pick-and-place success from 42.2% to 71.1% without lab-specific adaptation. These results suggest that world models for planning should preserve action-dependent differences needed for counterfactual selection, rather than optimize factual prediction accuracy alone. More videos and code are available at https://ad-wm.github.io/.

</details>


### [114] [Forecast-Dojo: Replayable Environments for Benchmarking and Training LLM Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28876)
*Liqin Ye,Haorui Wang,Fardin Ahmed,Rongzhi Zhang,Yuan He,Ziyuan Lin,Yanbin Yin,Jing Peng,Michael Galarnyk,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Forecast-Dojo 是一个可重放的环境，用于基准测试和训练大语言模型（LLM）的预测代理。它结合了已解决的预测市场问题和带日期的新闻，使代理能够在不同历史时间点回顾并更新其预测。该平台包含1,568个Polymarket事件和1880万篇带日期的新闻文章，支持重复评估、训练交互收集及结果反馈。实验表明，研究工具普遍降低所有12个模型的Brier分数，且随着事件进展，新证据的增加带来预测改进。尽管如此，所有模型仍落后于历史市场预测。携带信念笔记虽能降低研究成本，但不一致地提升预测质量。此外，Forecast-Dojo还为模型学习提供交互轨迹和结果反馈，以监督微调为例证明其潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型评估受限于真实世界事件的等待周期，缺乏可重复性和高效训练机制。需要一种能够持续评估、训练和反馈的环境，以提升大语言模型在时间序列预测任务中的表现。

Method: 构建Forecast-Dojo环境，整合历史预测市场数据与带日期新闻，支持多轮预测、研究与回溯；利用研究工具增强模型推理能力；通过监督微调验证环境在模型学习中的有效性。

Result: 研究工具显著降低12个模型的Brier分数；随着新证据积累，模型预测性能随时间改善；信念笔记减少研究成本但未稳定提升预测质量；所有模型仍不及历史市场预测水平。

Conclusion: Forecast-Dojo为大语言模型预测代理提供了可重复、高效、可反馈的训练与评估平台，具备推动模型学习与性能提升的潜力，但仍需进一步优化以接近市场基准。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-Dojo, a replayable environment for benchmarking and training LLM forecasting agents. It combines resolved prediction-market questions with dated news, allowing agents to research an event and revisit their predictions at successive historical dates. The same tasks and tools support repeated evaluation, collection of training interactions, and feedback from recorded outcomes without waiting for new events to resolve. Forecast-Dojo contains 1,568 Polymarket events, split by time into training and evaluation periods, and 18.8M dated news articles. In an evaluation of 12 models, research tools lower Brier score for all 12. Forecasts also improve as events unfold, with the largest gains at steps where more newly dated evidence is recorded. Every model still trails historical market forecasts in both Brier score and accuracy. A belief notebook carried between dates lowers research cost but does not consistently improve forecast quality. Beyond evaluation, Forecast-Dojo provides interaction trajectories and outcome feedback for agent learning, with supervised fine-tuning as a proof of concept.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Thinking Leakage: A Causal Audit of NoThink Post-Training in Hybrid Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28682)
*Zehao Liu,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了后训练混合推理模型在无思考（NoThink）模式下的性能提升是否源于模型自身已有的思考（Think）行为。通过因果中介框架和双向干预方法，发现存在显著的思考泄漏现象，即后训练模型的性能提升很大程度上是由于其向思考模式的偏移，而非真正提升了无思考能力。实验证明，沿着基础模型到衍生模型的激活方向进行引导，可重现大部分性能增益；而反向引导则会消除部分增益。在多个模型与方法中，思考泄漏比例达42%至79%，表明其具有显著的因果影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索后训练模型在无思考模式下性能提升的真实来源，识别是否存在对原有思考行为的依赖或重用，从而澄清这些改进是否真正提升了无思考能力。

Method: 采用因果中介分析框架，利用双向干预手段，在基础模型与衍生模型之间的简单激活方向上进行操作，通过行为与表征分析评估思考泄漏的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，思考泄漏真实存在且具有因果性，其贡献度高达42%至79%；沿激活方向引导可再现大部分性能提升，反向引导则显著削弱性能增益。

Conclusion: 后训练方法在无思考模式下的表现提升，可能并非真正增强了无思考能力，而是促使模型更倾向于使用原有的思考行为。因此，当前评估方式可能高估了真正的无思考能力进步。

Abstract: Post-training hybrid reasoning models in NoThink mode has attracted growing interest as a way to improve performance while keeping inference fast. However, these gains may draw on thinking behavior already accessible through the base model's Think mode. We formulate this thinking leakage in a causal mediation framework and audit its contribution using bidirectional interventions along a simple base-derived activation direction. Across three models and three post-training methods on competition math benchmarks, we find that leakage is real, causal, and substantial: behavioral and representational analyses reveal shifts toward Think, steering the base model along this direction reproduces most of the post-training accuracy gain, and counter-steering a checkpoint removes a substantial share of what it gains. Across nine aligned checkpoints with positive NoThink gains, the resulting leakage ratio ranges from 42% to 79%. These interventions support a substantial causal contribution of thinking leakage. Our findings show that a post-training method's apparent advantage can therefore reflect greater drift toward Think, obscuring whether it improves capability within NoThink or more effectively re-invokes existing Think behavior.

</details>


### [116] [CataOPD: Catalytic On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518)
*Wenjin Liu,Chenxi Wang,Jiapu Wang,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CataOPD提出一种新型强化学习与自洽推理结合的方法，通过教师作为催化剂扩展学生模型的推理路径，利用自救路由和催化引导自解决机制，提升复杂问题求解能力，并在不依赖教师的情况下实现更优的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习缺乏正确轨迹时无正向信号，而基于策略的蒸馏受限于学生模型当前策略可到达的轨迹范围；因此需要一种方法突破这些限制，提升大语言模型在复杂推理任务中的表现。

Method: CataOPD引入教师作为催化剂，而非直接目标；采用自救路由机制利用全部失败样本作为路由信号，进行额外自采样以寻找正确路径；对未解决的问题使用催化引导自解决，结合屏障加权内部化机制，聚焦于关键难解词元的更新。

Result: 实验表明，CataOPD优于现有基线，在未恢复问题上仍能独立推理，且在无催化剂推理下具备更强的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: CataOPD有效提升了大语言模型的自主推理能力，实现了无需依赖教师即可稳定输出高质量答案，为复杂任务求解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD) are two representative paradigms for improving large language model reasoning. However, when no correct trajectory is sampled, RL lacks a positive correctness signal, while OPD remains constrained by the reasoning trajectories reachable under the student's on-policy distribution. Therefore, we propose CataOPD, where the teacher acts as a catalyst rather than a target, expanding reachability while internalizing verified student-produced trajectories into a catalyst-free policy. Self-Rescue Routing uses empirically all-failed groups as routing signals rather than teacher-intervention triggers, first seeking correct trajectories through additional on-policy self-sampling. For problems unresolved after self-rescue, Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution uses catalytic guidance to elicit a verified student-produced trajectory in the guided student distribution. Barrier-Weighted Internalization weights tokens by guided-to-unguided log-probability gaps, focusing updates on decisive tokens difficult without guidance. Experimental results show that CataOPD outperforms current baselines, extends independent student reasoning to still-unrecovered problems, and improves out-of-distribution generalization under catalyst-free inference. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/CataOPD.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Average Safety: Chance-Constrained LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29960)
*Taha Entesari,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机会约束的安全性保持微调方法，通过限制安全性能下降超过阈值的样本比例，实现对罕见但严重失败的防护。引入可微分的近似方法处理不连续指标，设计了一种考虑约束的梯度下降法，使更新方向在保证安全性的同时最小化扰动，具有闭式解并强调接近或超过阈值的样本。在三个任务和三种模型上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有基线，表明安全性保持应被视为可靠性约束优化问题而非平均风险正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全性保持微调方法依赖平均安全损失或加权辅助惩罚，难以有效捕捉罕见但严重的失败情况，导致安全性能在关键时刻不可靠。

Method: 提出机会约束形式的安全性保持微调，用可微分的上界近似违规率，设计约束感知梯度下降算法，将约束视为参数空间中的安全集，并以闭式解形式进行尾部敏感的安全性修正。

Result: 在三个不同任务和三种模型上的大量实验表明，所提方法在保持安全性方面显著优于现有基线，尤其在防止严重退化方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 安全性保持在大语言模型微调中应被理解为可靠性约束优化问题，而非简单的平均风险正则化，所提方法能更有效地防范极端风险。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new objectives can improve helpfulness, instruction following, or domain-specific performance, but it can also induce regressions on safety-critical prompts. Existing safety-preserving fine-tuning methods typically control average safety loss or use weighted auxiliary penalties, which can obscure rare but severe failures. We propose a chance-constrained formulation for safety-preserving fine-tuning that limits the fraction of safety examples whose degradation relative to a reference model exceeds a prescribed threshold. Because the resulting empirical chance constraint contains a discontinuous indicator, we introduce a differentiable majorization of the violation rate, yielding a tractable conservative constraint. We then develop a constraint-aware gradient descent method that treats the majorized constraint as a safe set in parameter space and minimally modifies the fine-tuning direction to preserve feasibility. The resulting update admits a closed form and produces a tail-aware safety correction that emphasizes examples near or above the degradation threshold. We conduct an extensive set of experiments on harmful fine-tuning across three different tasks and three models and show that our approach consistently outperforms the baselines that exist in the literature. These results suggest that safety preservation in LLM fine-tuning is better viewed as a reliability-constrained optimization problem than as average-risk regularization.

</details>


### [118] [Let Training Guide Selection: Online Synthetic Data Filtering via Real-Anchored Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29988)
*Yanran Wu,Sana Lakdawala,Renzo Tassara Miller,Chongyang Bai,Sharath Ciddu,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Kungang Li,Sandeep Pandey,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FROST 是一种在线框架，通过基于真实训练数据的梯度反馈估计合成数据的效用，动态调整合成数据的筛选策略。它在不依赖外部验证集或保留集的情况下，仅在异常批次中过滤低效样本，从而提升模型性能。在图像分类、文本到SQL微调及大规模工业广告重排序系统中均表现出色，能有效过滤20-30%的合成数据并提升真实任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据选择方法多关注保真度或多样性，忽视了学习者在训练过程中的动态需求。当合成数据存在噪声和分布偏差时，其价值可能下降，亟需一种能根据实际训练反馈自适应筛选合成数据的方法。

Method: FROST利用真实训练数据的梯度反馈来估计合成数据的效用，通过与近期历史对比判断是否需要过滤，并仅在异常批次中进行筛选，避免对正常训练流程的干扰。该方法无需外部验证集或保留集，实现端到端的在线优化。

Result: 在图像分类和文本到SQL微调任务中，FROST成功过滤了约20-30%的合成数据，同时提升了真实任务性能；在大规模工业广告重排序系统中，显著优于高度优化的生产基线，证明其高效性与通用性。

Conclusion: FROST通过梯度驱动的动态筛选机制，有效解决了合成数据噪声与分布不匹配问题，在多种场景下均实现了性能提升，为合成数据的高效利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Synthetic data can scale training supervision when real-world data are limited, but noise and distribution mismatch can reduce its value. Existing synthetic data selection methods often emphasize fidelity or diversity rather than the learner's evolving needs. We propose FROST, an online framework that estimates synthetic-data utility through gradient feedback anchored in real training data. It calibrates batch utility against recent history to determine when filtering is needed and filters samples only in out-of-band batches to determine what to retain, without an external verifier or held-out validation set. Experiments on two public benchmarks for image classification and LLM fine-tuning for text-to-SQL show that FROST filters out around 20--30% of the synthetic data while improving real-task performance compared with training on the full synthetic data pool. We further apply FROST during training in a large-scale industrial ads re-ranking system, achieving significant performance gains over a highly optimized production baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [119] [Residual Correlation as a Diagnostic for Joint-Uncertainty Gains from GP Coregionalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30085)
*Fangqin Zhou,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.LG
