TL;DR: 本文研究多目标回归中通过多输出高斯过程与内在共区域化（GP-ICM）模型耦合相关目标的效果。发现核心收益在于联合不确定性量化，而非点预测；原始目标相关性无法预测耦合是否有效，而残差相关性（即独立建模后仍存在的跨目标依赖）是预测联合不确定性提升的最强指标。作者提出轻量级诊断工具 $D_{\rm logdet}$，基于独立GP计算，反映全协方差与对角协方差下理想化的负对数似然增益。在合成数据、16个基准任务及基于冻结神经网络的关节点回归中，点预测几乎不变（$ΔR^2≈0$），但 $D_{\rm logdet}$ 与实际NLL改进高度相关（$ρ_s=-0.83$, $p<0.001$），优于传统启发式方法。此外提出Residual-ICM，保持独立边际方差的同时引入残差相关结构，在联合NLL上表现最佳，并结合诊断工具判断耦合有效性。该诊断仅适用于可分离共区域化所捕捉的全局高斯残差依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标回归中，尽管使用多输出高斯过程与内在共区域化（GP-ICM）模型通过共享统计强度来耦合相关目标，但其实际性能提升并不稳定。现有方法缺乏可靠依据判断何时耦合真正有益，尤其在点预测方面效果有限。因此需要一种可解释、轻量且有效的诊断机制，以识别何时联合建模能带来真正的不确定性量化优势，而非徒增复杂性。

Method: 提出 $D_{\rm logdet} = -\frac{1}{2}\log\det R_{\rm res}$ 作为诊断指标，基于独立目标训练的残差协方差矩阵计算，无需联合建模即可评估潜在的联合不确定性改进空间。设计并比较了标准GP-ICM、独立模型、以及新提出的Residual-ICM模型——后者保留各目标独立边际方差，仅在残差部分引入共区域化结构。通过合成数据、真实基准和深度学习特征表示上的实验验证诊断有效性与模型性能。

Result: 在多种设置下，点预测性能基本无变化（$ΔR^2 \approx 0$），表明耦合主要影响不确定性估计。$D_{\rm logdet}$ 与实际联合负对数似然（NLL）改进高度相关（$ρ_s = -0.83$, $p < 0.001$），显著优于特征-样本比等启发式指标。Residual-ICM 在平均联合NLL上表现最优，同时诊断工具能准确指示耦合是否有效。结果表明，残差相关性是决定耦合价值的关键因素。

Conclusion: 多输出高斯过程中的核心区域化主要提升联合不确定性量化能力，而非点预测精度。残差相关性是判断耦合是否有效的关键指标。提出的 $D_{\rm logdet}$ 诊断工具能高效预判联合建模收益，而Residual-ICM模型在保持预测稳定性的同时实现最优不确定性建模，为实际应用提供实用指导。

Abstract: In multi-target regression, correlated targets are often coupled through multi-output Gaussian processes with an intrinsic model of coregionalisation (GP-ICM), assuming that sharing statistical strength improves overall performance. In practice, the benefits are inconsistent. Across the settings studied, we find that the main benefit of coregionalisation is joint uncertainty quantification rather than point prediction. Raw target correlation does not predict when coupling helps; in the separable GP-ICM settings studied here, residual correlation, the cross-target dependence left unexplained by independent per-target predictors, is the strongest predictor of joint-uncertainty gains.
  We introduce a lightweight diagnostic, $D_{\rm logdet}=-\frac{1}{2}\log\det R_{\rm res}$, which represents the idealised joint negative log-likelihood (NLL) gain from modelling a full rather than diagonal residual covariance and is computable from independent GPs alone. Across a controlled synthetic study, 16 multi-target benchmarks, and frozen transformer and convolutional neural network representations for keypoint regression, point prediction remains largely unchanged ($ΔR^2\approx 0$). In contrast, $D_{\rm logdet}$ strongly predicts observed ICM NLL improvements ($ρ_s=-0.83$, $p<0.001$), outperforming heuristics such as the feature-to-sample ratio. We also propose Residual-ICM, which preserves independent marginal variances while adding residual-correlation structure to the joint covariance. Residual-ICM achieves the best average joint NLL among the compared methods, while the diagnostic indicates when covariance coupling is likely to be useful. The diagnostic is specific to global Gaussian residual dependence, the structure captured by separable coregionalisation.

</details>


### [120] [AT-SKM-Net: An Accelerated Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Framework for Linear Hard-Constraint Feasibility on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30088)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yao Zhang,Qingchun Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AT-SKM Net框架通过混合采样策略和乔列斯基更新机制，显著提升了图结构优化中线性约束处理的效率与可扩展性，实验表明其迭代次数减少高达85%，SKM层速度提升2.95至7.29倍，同时保证零约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投影的方法在动态环境中因需处理全部约束集和昂贵的矩阵分解而计算成本高，难以应对大规模、高维度的严格硬约束问题。

Method: 提出加速可训练SKM（AT-SKM）Net框架，采用拓扑感知异质图神经网络引导的混合采样策略聚焦活跃约束，结合乔列斯基更新机制降低等式投影复杂度，实现高效处理拓扑变化。

Result: 在随机几何图、N-1安全约束直流最优潮流及最小成本天然气输运问题上，AT-SKM显著减少迭代次数（最高85%），实现2.95x-7.29x的SKM层加速，且保持零约束违反。

Conclusion: AT-SKM Net有效解决了图结构优化中高维硬约束带来的可扩展性难题，为关键基础设施优化提供了高效、可靠的新方法。

Abstract: Graph-structured optimization with linear constraints is fundamental to critical infrastructure but faces scalability limits due to massive strict hard constraints and high dimensionality. While recent projection-based methods such as Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Net (T-SKM-Net) guarantee feasibility, they face high computational costs in dynamic environments by processing the entire constraint set and requiring expensive matrix factorizations. To bridge this gap, we propose the Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net framework. To concentrate computation on the active constraints and eliminate redundant calculations, we introduce a hybrid sampling strategy guided by a topology-aware heterogeneous GNN model. To efficiently handle topological shifts in graph-based constraints, we employ a Cholesky Update mechanism that theoretically reduces the equality projection complexity from O(N^3) to O(N^2) under low-rank perturbations. Experiments on random geometric graphs, N-1 Security-Constrained DC-OPF, and minimum-cost gas transport problem demonstrate that AT-SKM reduces iteration counts by up to 85% and achieves 2.95x-7.29x SKM layer speedups, while maintaining zero constraint violations.

</details>


### [121] [On the SoS Certifiability of Log-Concave Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30105)
*Aleksandr Storozhenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对于任意各向同性对数凹分布 $P$，多项式 $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \\mathbb{E}_{X\\sim P}\\langle X,v\\rangle^m$ 在每个偶数 $m\\ge2$ 时均为平方和，其中 $C>0$ 为通用常数。该结果消除了原定理中对庞加莱常数的依赖，恢复了对数凹分布的最优矩界，并导出了一系列具有维度无关误差保证的高效计算算法，适用于广泛的高维统计估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论中对数凹分布的矩界依赖于庞加莱常数，限制了其在高维场景下的应用。本文旨在去除这一依赖，获得更优、更普适的矩界，从而推动高维统计估计中可计算算法的发展。

Method: 采用随机局部化技术将分布 $P$ 分解为一系列随机强对数凹测度的平均；利用 Diakonikolas 等人关于中心矩的次高斯证书，结合 Letwin 的二次型方差不等式，通过协方差自适应的局部化选择，控制各阶偶数矩的平均行为，最终证明多项式为平方和。

Result: 证明了对于任意偶数 $m\\ge2$，$(Cm)^m\\|v\\|_2^m - \\mathbb{E}_{X\\sim P}\\langle X,v\\rangle^m$ 是平方和，从而得到最优矩界；并由此导出维度无关误差的高效计算算法。

Conclusion: 本工作通过随机局部化与子高斯证书的结合，实现了对数凹分布矩界的最优刻画，显著提升了高维统计估计中算法的可计算性与理论保障，为相关领域提供了新的工具与视角。

Abstract: For an arbitrary isotropic log-concave distribution $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, we prove that the polynomial $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ is a sum of squares for every even $m\ge2$, where $C>0$ is a universal constant. This removes the dependence on the Poincaré constant in the theorem of Kothari and Steinhardt (arXiv:1711.07465), recovering the optimal moment bounds for log-concave distributions. As an immediate corollary, we obtain computationally efficient algorithms with dimension-free error guarantees for a wide range of high-dimensional statistical estimation problems.
  Our proof uses stochastic localization to decompose $P$ as an average of random strongly log-concave measures, whose centered moments admit the subgaussian certificates of Diakonikolas, Hopkins, Pensia, and Tiegel (STOC 2025; arXiv:2410.21194). With a covariance-adapted choice of localization, we show that a fourth-moment certificate derived from Letwin's variance inequality for quadratic forms (arXiv:2607.24164) suffices to control this averaging at every even degree.

</details>


### [122] [Graph-Based Inference and Topology-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Large-Scale Railway Network Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30150)
*Giacomo Arcieri,Gregory Duthé,Christophe Muller,Konstantinos G. Papakonstantinou,Daniel Straub,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的框架，用于解决铁路维护规划中的大规模基础设施资产管理问题。通过层次化贝叶斯模型与高斯过程图核结合，实现对真实世界数据中空间相关性的精准环境建模；并引入拓扑感知的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，利用图神经网络和图Transformer优化网络级策略。关键贡献在于实现了零样本迁移学习：在小规模网络上训练的图智能体可直接部署于大规模未见网络而无需重新训练。数值实验表明，该方法在显著降低训练时间的同时，性能优于优化启发式算法和标准MARL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法在处理大规模基础设施网络时面临计算不可行性或缺乏协调机制的问题，亟需一种既能保持系统级协同又具备可扩展性的解决方案。

Method: 采用层次化贝叶斯模型结合高斯过程图核进行空间相关环境建模，并设计拓扑感知的多智能体强化学习框架，集成图神经网络与图Transformer以实现网络级决策优化；通过零样本迁移学习实现跨规模部署。

Result: 所提方法在大规模网络上显著优于优化启发式算法与标准MARL基线，在降低计算训练时间的同时保持优异性能，验证了其高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该图基框架成功解决了大规模基础设施维护决策中的可扩展性与协调性难题，为实际工程应用提供了强有力的技术支持。

Abstract: Modern infrastructure asset management constitutes a complex sequential decision-making problem, characterized by long planning horizons and system-level interactions, such as spatial deterioration correlations and economies of scale. While deep reinforcement learning has shown promise in optimizing maintenance policies, scaling to real-world networks remains challenging. Centralized approaches become computationally intractable in large-scale systems, whereas decentralized approaches often fail to capture essential coordination mechanisms. To address these challenges, we propose a graph-based framework that integrates accurate environment modeling with scalable decision support. First, we employ a hierarchical Bayesian model leveraging a Gaussian Process on Graph kernel to infer a realistic, spatially correlated networked environment of railway maintenance planning from real-world data provided by the Swiss Federal Railways. Second, we introduce a topology-aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework by integrating graph neural networks and graph Transformers to optimize network-level policies. A central contribution of this work is the demonstration of scalability through zero-shot transfer learning: graph-based agents, trained only on small network portions, are successfully deployed in a zero-shot manner on large-scale unseen networks without any retraining. Numerical results indicate that the proposed method significantly outperforms optimized heuristics and standard MARL baselines, reducing computational training time while maintaining superior performance on large-scale networks.

</details>


### [123] [Intrinsic-Extrinsic Coupling in Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30185)
*Qinyou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了内在-外在耦合在连续学习中的作用，提出通过约束学习状态干预的延续条件价值来建模。利用有限帧分类头保护当前逻辑，修复历史边界，并通过对比实验揭示了内在干预与外部延续之间的非加性交互。在增量学习设置下，重放机制使更新贡献从5次正确预测降至0；输出蒸馏和优化器原生SGDW动态下也存在非零交互，且在128步更新时呈现负向交互，表明耦合不必然带来正向协同。数学分析区分了可行局部修复与理想终端输出，协调测试显示内容控制可匹配或超越开发增益，五根路径实验表明正确计数效应依赖于根节点而非普遍有益。交叉熵读出中，引导分配的平均损失低于标准重放。整体结果将内在-外在耦合操作化，连接状态几何与延续条件价值，实现匹配交互识别与闭环协调，同时分离耦合与完整策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在持续学习中如何有效管理内在（学习状态）与外在（训练延续）因素之间的相互作用，以提升模型在增量任务下的表现。尤其关注如何在有限精度条件下修复历史错误，避免灾难性遗忘，同时保持对新任务的适应能力。

Method: 提出一种基于延续条件价值的约束学习状态干预框架，使用有限帧分类头保护当前输出，结合观察相对纤维描述一致性，并通过四单元对比实验识别非加性交互。采用CLINC类增量学习设置，评估重放、输出蒸馏及SGDW优化动态下的性能变化，进行多路径根节点测试与引导分配策略比较。

Result: 重放机制使32次更新的正确预测数从5降为0；在输出蒸馏和SGDW下仍存在显著非零交互，且在128步更新时出现负向正确计数交互。内容控制在多数情况下表现优于或等于开发增益，五根路径实验显示正确计数效应具有根依赖性。交叉熵读出中，引导分配策略的平均损失低于标准重放。

Conclusion: 内在-外在耦合可通过状态几何与延续条件价值的连接实现操作化，支持匹配交互识别与闭环协调，但其效果独立于完整策略性能。耦合不必然带来正向协同，需谨慎设计干预机制以避免负面影响。

Abstract: A learner's current observations need not determine its response to further training. We formulate intrinsic-extrinsic coupling through the continuation-conditioned value of a constrained learning-state intervention, with observation-relative fibers describing present agreement. An executable finite-frame classifier-head write protects current logits while repairing specified historical margins under finite-precision acceptance checks. We distinguish local admissibility, continuation-conditioned intervention value, and complete-policy performance. A matched four-cell contrast identifies readout-specific non-additivity between the same intrinsic intervention and alternative external continuations. In a CLINC-derived class-incremental setting, replay changes the write's 32-update contribution from five correct predictions to zero. Nonzero interactions also occur under output distillation, with a RoBERTa backbone, and under optimizer-native SGDW dynamics. Under SGDW, correct-count interactions are negative in all three activated roots at 128 updates, showing that coupling need not imply positive synergy. The mathematical analysis distinguishes feasible local repairs and favorable terminal outputs from training-reachable repair regions. Separate coordination tests show that content controls match or exceed the development gain, while a five-root fresh-test comparison with Fiber present in every arm shows root-dependent rather than uniformly beneficial correct-count effects. On the secondary cross-entropy readout, guided allocation yields lower mean loss than standard replay in all five pairs. Together, these results make intrinsic-extrinsic coupling operational by connecting executable state geometry to continuation-conditioned value, matched interaction identification, and closed-loop coordination, while separating identified coupling from complete-policy performance.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Compression: Training Latent Representations for Stable Long-Horizon Rollout in Neural Surrogate Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30198)
*Andreas E. Robertson,Ashley T. Lenau,John D. Shimanek,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Vivek Oommen,David L. Damm,Krishna Garikipati,Remi Dingreville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过改进训练策略来增强潜在动态模型在长时间预测中的稳定性，而非依赖于原始的重建目标。通过引入Koopman算子学习、汉明噪声注入和多步滚动微调等方法，显著降低了长期滚动预测误差约40%，在两个物理基准测试中达到或超过全分辨率模型的精度，同时大幅降低计算资源需求（减少100倍浮点运算量，使用一半GPU内存）。该方法成功应用于高循环疲劳的介观晶体塑性模拟，实现了远超训练范围的稳定外推。研究表明，神经压缩应不仅降低维度，更需重构解空间以支持稳定的动力学演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动态模型在长时间自回归滚动预测中误差累积严重，限制了其实际应用。尽管潜在表示理论上能降低成本并简化学习，但仅以重建为目标的训练方式导致表示不适合长时序预测。因此需要重新设计训练策略，使潜在表示更适合长期动力学演化。

Method: 采用多种训练干预措施：在自编码器训练中引入Koopman算子学习与汉明噪声注入以改善压缩能力；在动态建模阶段加入噪声注入与多步滚动微调，提升长期滚动稳定性。这些方法旨在将潜在表示对齐于长时序预测任务。

Result: 所提方法使长期滚动预测误差降低约40%，在两个物理基准上表现优于或等同于全分辨率模型，且计算成本下降两个数量级（浮点运算减少100倍），GPU内存占用减半。在晶体塑性疲劳模拟中实现了对远超训练范围的时间尺度的稳定外推。

Conclusion: 神经压缩不应仅关注降维，而应主动重构解空间，使其支持稳定的动力学演化。这一原则对构建高效、可靠的科学计算神经代理模型至关重要。

Abstract: Latent neural surrogate solvers, or latent dynamics models, accelerate simulations of time-dependent physical systems by evolving a compressed latent space rather than resolving full-resolution fields directly. In principle this reduces computational cost and simplifies learning, but in practice errors often accumulate rapidly during long autoregressive rollouts, limiting predictive utility. We show that this instability does not stem from the latent representation itself, but arises when it is trained solely for reconstruction, producing representations poorly suited to long-horizon forecasting. We systematically evaluate training-level interventions that align latent representations with long-horizon rollout: Koopman operator learning and Hamming noise injection during autoencoder training to improve compression, together with noise injection and multi-step rollout fine-tuning to improve dynamics. Interventions that improve long-horizon rollout stability often degrade conventional training metrics, including reconstruction and one-step prediction accuracy. Collectively, these interventions reduce long-rollout error by approximately 40\% and match or exceed the accuracy of full-resolution models on two physics benchmarks, while requiring 2 orders of magnitude fewer floating point operations and half the GPU memory. Applied to mesoscale crystal-plasticity simulations of high-cycle fatigue, the resulting surrogate achieves stable extrapolation over horizons orders of magnitude beyond those observed during training. More broadly, these results show that neural compression should be designed not merely to reduce dimensionality, but to restructure the solution space for stable dynamical evolution, a key requirement for reliable, efficient neural surrogates in scientific applications.

</details>


### [125] [Minimally Invasive Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30218)
*Taha Entesari,Jingyu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MISVO 是一种在测试时通过向语言模型的最终隐藏状态添加向量来适应奖励的方法，通过利用局部 KL 几何结构惩罚干预，以最小化对输出分布的扰动。该方法引入 Fisher 二次项作为分布敏感性的度量，并能通过矩阵-向量乘积计算解析梯度。研究推导出序列级 KL 梯度的精确分解，证明后缀项在干预幅度上为二阶小量，且三种 Fisher 近似与完整 KL 梯度在首阶一致。MISVO 使用冻结参考近似优化位置特定干预，无需更新模型参数。在多个 1B–14B 参数的语言模型上，于偏好和代码生成任务中，在七组设置中有六组达到最高平均奖励，同时保持与 Best-of-N 相当的多样性和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预逻辑调制方法在未正则化的奖励优化下可能显著改变输出分布，导致生成质量下降。需要一种在不更新模型参数的前提下，有效控制干预强度并保持生成质量的方法。

Method: 提出 Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization (MISVO)，基于局部 KL 几何构造 Fisher 二次惩罚项，实现解析梯度计算；推导序列级 KL 梯度的分解，分离出 Fisher 主导项与后缀修正项；使用冻结参考近似进行位置感知的干预优化。

Result: 在 1B–14B 参数规模的模型上，六种任务设置中获得最高平均奖励；生成多样性与连贯性接近 Best-of-N 方法，表明其在性能与稳定性之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: MISVO 通过利用 KL 几何结构实现对语言模型输出分布的轻量、精准调控，在不更新模型参数的情况下显著提升生成质量与奖励表现，适用于多种下游任务。

Abstract: Pre-logit steering adapts a frozen language model to a test-time reward by adding vectors to its final hidden states. Unregularized reward optimization can substantially alter the output distribution and degrade generation quality. We propose Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization (MISVO), which penalizes interventions using the local KL geometry of the induced token distribution. The resulting Fisher quadratic measures distributional sensitivity and admits an analytic gradient computed through matrix--vector products with the frozen language-model head. We derive an exact decomposition of the sequence-level KL gradient into an analytic Fisher term and a suffix score-function term. For a fixed generation horizon, we show that the suffix term is second order in the steering magnitude and that three Fisher surrogates agree with the full KL gradient to first order. MISVO uses the frozen-reference surrogate to optimize position-specific interventions without updating model parameters. Across preference and code-generation tasks on models with approximately 1B--14B parameters, MISVO achieves the highest mean reward in six of seven model--task settings, with diversity and coherence scores close to those of Best-of-N.

</details>


### [126] [PoEM: Predicting RL Outcomes from Existing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30226)
*Kimia Hamidieh,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PoEM框架，通过已训练的模型预测新奖励函数下的强化学习（RL）结果，避免重复进行计算密集且不稳定的RL训练。研究发现，即使奖励函数非线性相关，其对数策略在跨奖励下仍近似低秩，可通过现有模型输出估计权重系数，实现无需实际运行RL即可逼近目标策略。实验验证了该方法在文本和图像模态上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于强化学习的后训练过程计算成本高、不稳定，且每次奖励模型变化或需组合多个奖励时都需从头开始训练。因此，迫切需要一种无需重新运行RL即可预测新奖励下策略的方法。

Method: 提出PoEM框架，利用已有后训练模型和新奖励函数，通过观察到的对数策略在奖励空间中近似低秩的特性，仅基于奖励或基础策略输出即可估计线性组合权重，从而构建新策略的近似表示。

Result: 在合成与真实奖励函数上，无论文本还是图像模态，该方法均能有效预测强化学习结果，显著减少对额外训练的需求，且预测性能接近真实RL训练结果。

Conclusion: PoEM框架成功实现了对新奖励函数下强化学习结果的高效预测，证明了对数策略在不同奖励间具有可线性组合的潜在结构，为减少强化学习训练开销提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation models are post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to maximize specific rewards, such as human alignment, correctness, or instruction following. This post-training process is computationally intensive, sometimes unstable, and has to be run from scratch every time the reward model changes or when we want to combine multiple rewards. We hence ask: given a new reward function, is it possible to predict the RL outcomes without actually running RL on it? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing PoEM, a framework to predict the outputs of RL on a new reward function using a set of models already post-trained on other rewards. First, we show that if the new reward function can be written as a linear combination of existing ones, then the new policy in log-space can be written as a linear combination of the existing log-policies. Surprisingly, even in cases where the rewards are not linearly connected, we observe that often log-policies from RL training span an approximately low-rank subspace across rewards. To our benefit, the weighting coefficients for this combination can be estimated using only the reward or basis policy outputs on the samples. We turn these observations into an algorithm that takes post-trained models and a new reward function, and approximates the target RL policy without actually running any additional RL training. We experimentally validate our approach across synthetic and real rewards, spanning both text and image modalities.

</details>


### [127] [To Trust or Not to Trust: Retrieval-Augmented Fact Checking in Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30227)
*Debajyoti Mazumder,Mamta,Abhirama Subramanyam Penamakuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriSpeak is a benchmark for speech-based fact verification in Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), containing 3,879 spoken claims across temporal, geographical, and relational facts with balanced true/false labels. It evaluates whether factual verification transfers from text to speech and whether retrieval-augmented LALMs can correctly use textual evidence to verify spoken claims. Experiments show a consistent modality gap: LALMs perform well on written claims but fail on the same claims when spoken. Retrieval alone helps little due to model confusion between retrieved evidence and the claim. However, combining retrieval with explicit reasoning significantly improves performance, with a thinking-tuned LALM achieving 86.1% accuracy. The study emphasizes that effective speech misinformation detection requires both strong speech understanding and grounded reasoning over evidence.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rise of online misinformation in spoken formats like news clips, podcasts, and social media videos necessitates fact-checking systems capable of verifying claims directly from speech. Existing systems primarily focus on text-based verification, leaving a critical gap in handling spoken content. This work aims to bridge that gap by developing a benchmark to study how well Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) can perform fact verification on spoken claims and whether knowledge transfer from text to speech is effective.

Method: The authors introduce VeriSpeak, a probe benchmark consisting of 3,879 spoken claims covering temporal, geographical, and relational facts with balanced truth labels. They evaluate LALMs on their ability to verify these claims, comparing performance between text and speech modalities. They also test retrieval-augmented models using textual evidence and assess the impact of explicit reasoning via 'thinking' mechanisms. Experiments include baseline performance, retrieval-only setups, and retrieval-with-reasoning approaches.

Result: A consistent text-speech modality gap is observed: LALMs that reliably verify written claims often fail when the same claims are spoken. Retrieval alone yields limited improvement because models frequently confuse retrieved evidence with the spoken claim. In contrast, retrieval combined with explicit reasoning significantly enhances performance, with a thinking-tuned LALM achieving 86.1% accuracy. This indicates that grounding reasoning over evidence is crucial for accurate speech-based fact verification.

Conclusion: Effective detection of speech-based misinformation requires more than just speech understanding—it demands robust, grounded reasoning over retrieved evidence. The findings underscore the importance of designing LALMs with explicit reasoning capabilities to overcome modality-specific challenges in spoken fact verification.

Abstract: Online misinformation increasingly appears in spoken formats such as news clips, podcasts, interviews, political speeches, and social media videos, creating a need for fact-checking systems that can verify claims directly from speech. We introduce VeriSpeak, a probe benchmark for studying speech-based fact verification in Large Audio Language Models (LALMs). VeriSpeak contains 3,879 spoken claims spanning temporal, geographical, and relational facts, with balanced true and false labels. The benchmark is designed to examine whether factual verification ability transfers from text to speech, and whether retrieval-augmented LALMs can use textual evidence to correctly support or refute spoken claims. Our experiments reveal a consistent text-speech modality gap: LALMs that verify written claims reliably often fail on the same claims when spoken. Moreover, retrieval alone provides limited gains because models frequently conflate retrieved evidence with the spoken claim. In contrast, retrieval combined with explicit reasoning improves claim-evidence comparison, with a thinking-tuned LALM reaching 86.1% accuracy. VeriSpeak highlights that effective speech misinformation detection requires not only speech understanding, but also grounded reasoning over retrieved evidence. The dataset is publicly available via Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/abhiram4572/VeriSpeak.

</details>


### [128] [Temporal Gradient Inversion for Private Trajectory Reconstruction in Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30258)
*Sudip Bhujel,Shanghao Shi,Ruiquan Huang,Ning Zhang,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TRACE的新型时序梯度反演攻击，可从分布式强化学习中的政策梯度中重建私有观测-动作轨迹序列。该方法利用时间相关性和策略头梯度结构，实现了高保真度重建（18.8 dB PSNR），且速度远超现有方法，适用于多种架构和输入模态，揭示了现有隐私保护机制在时序数据上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式强化学习方案虽通过本地保留传感器数据提升隐私，但时序结构仍可能被利用进行梯度反演攻击；以往方法仅关注单帧信息，忽略连续梯度间的时序关联与动作恢复的精确性，因此亟需更全面的攻击评估以推动更强的隐私防御机制。

Method: TRACE采用自回归方式，基于条件互信息界建模跨时间梯度相关性，并利用策略头梯度中封闭形式的动作恢复特性，在小熵正则条件下实现精确动作重构，结合高效优化策略完成序列重建。

Result: 在真实场景测试中，TRACE实现18.8 dB PSNR，动作恢复近乎完美，每帧重建耗时仅3-4.5毫秒，优于所有学习型与优化型基线方法，且在多架构、多模态和大离散动作空间下表现稳定。

Conclusion: 当前分布式强化学习中的梯度传输存在显著时序隐私泄露风险，单纯依赖单帧保护已不足够；未来隐私防护必须引入序列感知机制，才能有效应对如TRACE所示的高级时序攻击。

Abstract: Distributed learning in embodied reinforcement-learning agents offers a degree of privacy by retaining raw sensor data on-device and transmitting only policy gradients to the server. Yet temporal structure can amplify this leakage beyond single-frame attacks. We introduce Temporal Reconstruction Attack on Consecutive Encodings (TRACE), an amortized temporal gradient-inversion attack that autoregressively reconstructs the sequence of private observation-action trajectories from per-step policy-learning gradients. The attack exploits two structural signals ignored by prior single-frame methods: (i) cross-time correlation between successive embodied gradients, which we formalize via a conditional mutual-information bound, and (ii) closed-form action recovery from policy-head gradient structure, which we prove exact when standard entropy regularization is sufficiently small. On held-out embodied scenes, TRACE reaches $18.8$ dB PSNR with near-perfect action recovery at $3$-$4.5$ ms per reconstructed frame, dominating the learning-based baseline across all reconstruction metrics and exceeding optimization attacks while running orders of magnitude faster. Further evaluation demonstrates TRACE's broader applicability across recurrent, residual, and compact transformer victim architectures, multi-modal inputs, and larger discrete action spaces. Defense experiments suggest that protecting temporal gradient streams may require sequence-aware privacy mechanisms.

</details>


### [129] [Graph, Loop, and Harness Engineering for Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering and Analytical Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29668)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种零信任框架：零信任代理数据工程与零信任代理OLAP，实现从自然语言任务到云端数据工程与分析的自动化，通过证据约束、验证与可追溯性确保安全与可靠性。框架共享图工程、循环工程与代理-框架工程三大抽象，支持在正常执行、故障恢复、策略约束等场景下的验证完成度、恢复能力、授权控制与生产发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型代理虽能自动化数据工作流，但缺乏对代码、数据、基础设施及运行环境的可靠协调机制，尤其在生产环境中存在信任风险。为解决这一问题，需构建零信任机制以确保端到端数据工程与分析的可信执行。

Method: 提出两个零信任框架：零信任代理数据工程（基于证据的生成、部署与验证）与零信任代理OLAP（结合受控数据准备与验证的OLAP执行）。采用图工程管理证据驱动的工作流结构，循环工程实现有限恢复，代理-框架工程保障零信任执行。所有操作均依赖证据链和严格验证。

Result: 实验表明，两种框架在正常执行、可控故障、有限恢复及策略约束条件下均实现了高可信度的验证完成、系统恢复、权限控制与生产发布。验证的OLAP执行满足同快照执行、结果等价、确定性归因与反思机制，确保输出可信赖。

Conclusion: 本研究通过引入零信任原则与三大核心抽象，实现了数据工程与分析流程的可信自动化，为大规模云原生数据工作流的安全部署与执行提供了可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly automate data workflows, but end-to-end cloud data engineering and analytical execution require reliable coordination across code, data, infrastructure, and runtime environments. We present two zero-trust frameworks. Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering generates, deploys, and verifies complete cloud data-engineering solutions from natural-language tasks, with completion conditioned on repository, deployment, runtime, and policy evidence. Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP combines governed Data Preparation with verified Online Analytical Processing (OLAP), permitting production promotion only after validation and evidence-bound approval, and releasing analytical answers only after Same-Snapshot Execution, Exact Result Equivalence, deterministic grounding, and reflection. Both frameworks share three abstractions: graph engineering for evidence-gated workflow structure, loop engineering for bounded recovery, and agent-harness engineering for zero-trust execution. We evaluate both frameworks under nominal execution, controlled failures, bounded recovery, and policy-constrained conditions, measuring verified completion, recovery, authorization enforcement, production promotion, and verified OLAP execution.

</details>
